### 2026-06-11 08:15:40  #資料 #市場 #觀點

【中信建投：6月行業配置建議採用“槓鈴結構”】中信建投研報指出，6月行業配置建議採用“槓鈴結構”：一端保留AI、半導體和出口製造等高景氣底倉，另一端配置高股息、現金流穩定資產控制組合波動，中間用週期漲價、新能源修復和政策訂單主題做彈性倉位。5月市場主線高度集中在通訊、電子、AI硬體鏈，說明資金仍圍繞產業景氣和業績兌現定價，但AI鏈短期漲幅較大、交易擁擠度上升，6月不宜繼續提升AI總倉位，內部應從高漲幅環節切向半導體材料、裝置零部件、PCB材料、國產算力配套等滯漲且基本面改善方向。若PPI繼續修復、收入—庫存差改善，則週期漲價鏈權重可適度提高，從煤炭、有色逐步擴散至化工、機械、交運等方向，但前提是價格上漲能夠傳導至收入和毛利率。

### 2026-06-11 08:15:10  #Trading Economics #Economy #Argentina #Credit Rating #Importance 1

S&P Raises Argentina's Credit Rating to B- — Credit ratings agency S&P Global upgraded Argentina's long-term sovereign credit rating to 'B-' from 'CCC+' on Wednesday, citing easing economic vulnerabilities and gradually improving external liquidity. However, the agency warned that the rating remains constrained by still-low net foreign-currency reserves, a history of policy volatility, and high levels of government debt relative to peers. S&P expects Argentina's economy to continue recovering after emerging from recession, supported by lower inflation, rising real wages, and a gradual recovery in domestic demand. Meanwhile, improved access to external financing, including multilateral funding and a gradual return to capital markets, should support foreign-exchange reserve accumulation and debt repayments. Moody's credit rating for Argentina was last set at Caa1 with a stable outlook. DBRS' credit rating for Argentina was last reported at B (low) with a stable outlook.

### 2026-06-11 08:11:27  #市場

【申萬宏源：A股在6至7月可能快速調整，下半年還有上漲行情】“目前，A股結構性上漲行情演繹已接近高位區域，而全面上漲空間尚待開啟。6月至7月可能是快速調整視窗。”6月10日，申萬宏源研究A股策略首席分析師傅靜濤表示。傅靜濤是在申萬宏源2026資本市場夏季策略會上作出的上述表述。但與此同時，傅靜濤認為，從宏觀環境、AI產業趨勢、增量資金流入格局三個角度看，當前A股還不具備大週期上行出現大級別波折的條件。傅靜濤表示，雖然6月至7月可能是快速調整視窗，但隨著後續全面上漲開啟空間，更多結構融入大週期上行行情，需要行情拉長時間，拓展大週期上行的邏輯縱深，2026年下半年還有新一輪上漲行情的判斷不變。（澎湃）

### 2026-06-11 08:05:20  #央行 #美聯儲

【中金：維持美聯儲年內不降息、不加息的基準判斷】中金認為，當前通脹仍以能源衝擊等結構性因素為主，週期性通脹尚不明顯，但需警惕AI資本開支擴張與就業改善帶來的總需求回升風險。貨幣政策上，中金維持美聯儲年內不降息、不加息的基準判斷。預計聯儲態度將保持鷹派，沃什上任後首要任務是重建政策公信力，更可能透過強化縮表預期而非暗示加息來展示決心。

---

### 2026-06-11 08:05:12  #資料 #觀點

【中信證券：上游漲價正在逐漸向下擴散】中信證券指出，5月PPI同比回升至3.9%，再次超出市場預期。雖然原油價格邊際上有所回落，但化工、有色、電子、鋼鐵、煤炭行業的價格貢獻了較強的向上動能。5月CPI同比持平於前置的1.2%，略低於市場預期，核心CPI同比有所回落，“能源”與“服務”價格偏弱或為主要驅動因素。相關分項中，能源、交通工具租賃、飛機票CPI表現偏弱；旅行社及其他旅遊服務、衣著、通訊工具CPI表現較強。當前市場普遍關注國內輸入型通脹對經濟的影響路徑。向後看，6月PPI-CPI剪刀差可能持續保持高位，下半年伴隨PPI高基數效應顯現PPI斜率或有所放緩，高景氣行業的順價能力或較強，但需求偏弱行業的順價不暢問題或將逐漸顯現。若國際原油價格持續高位，我國規模以上工業企業利潤增速或將呈現出“先上升、後下降”的走勢特徵。債市策略方面，在資金寬鬆的大背景下，債市近期整體偏強執行。消費端政策增量明確前，預計市場對本輪通脹上行的定價可能更多停留在輸入性、成本端的視角上，長債利率趨勢性回升風險也較為有限。

---

### 2026-06-11 08:04:42  #資料 #市場 #觀點 #央行 #國際

【中金：維持美聯儲年內不降息、不加息的基準判斷】中金指出，美國5月CPI季調環比上漲0.5%，同比上漲4.2%，漲幅為2023年以來最高水平；核心CPI環比上漲0.2%，同比上漲2.9%。本次通脹上行主要由能源分項推動，汽油、燃油領漲，機票、快遞服務等傳導效應繼續顯現，但未廣泛擴散。核心通脹整體溫和，汽車等對油價敏感領域的需求受到擠壓，房租漲幅亦明顯收窄。中金認為，當前通脹仍以能源衝擊等結構性因素為主，週期性通脹尚不明顯，但需警惕AI資本開支擴張與就業改善帶來的總需求回升風險。貨幣政策上，中金維持美聯儲年內不降息、不加息的基準判斷。預計聯儲態度將保持鷹派，沃什上任後首要任務是重建政策公信力，更可能透過強化縮表預期而非暗示加息來展示決心。不排除出現“縮表先行、降息推遲”的格局，對與沃什的理念格格不入、依賴流動性驅動、受益於美元超發的資產構成持續壓力。

---

### 2026-06-11 08:03:47  #國際

【中國代表就推進中東和平提出四點主張】中國常駐聯合國代表傅聰10日在安理會“推進中東和平：斡旋與對話促進持久和平”公開辯論會上發言，就推進中東和平提出四點主張。

傅聰說，當前中東局勢持續緊張，不僅衝擊地區和平穩定，也直接影響世界經濟和能源安全，損害各國共同利益。國際社會應當秉持公正立場，匯聚促和努力，早日還中東以穩定，還人民以安寧，還世界以和平。他就此提出四點主張。

一是堅持政治解決，以和平方式化解爭端。各方應當恪守聯合國憲章宗旨和原則，堅持透過對話協商彌合分歧，避免使用或威脅使用武力。安理會要履行維護國際和平與安全的首要職責，採取積極和負責任的行動，推動熱點問題政治解決。中方呼籲伊朗局勢相關各方保持冷靜剋制，停止可能激化衝突的對抗行動，堅持透過政治外交途徑化解爭端。中方敦促黎以局勢有關各方立即停止敵對行動，真正實現停火止戰。

二是堅持公平正義，以國際法治維護地區穩定。中東熱點問題延宕不決，重要原因就在於公道正義沒有得到伸張，安理會相關決議沒有得到落實，國際法治沒有得到遵守。必須維護安理會和國際法權威，不能“合則用、不合則棄”，不能讓世界退回叢林法則。國際社會要匯聚更大努力落實“兩國方案”，拒絕任何吞併巴勒斯坦領土的企圖，支援巴勒斯坦早日獨立建國併成為聯合國正式會員國。

三是堅持獨立自主，以團結合作構建共同安全。中東由亂向治的根本出路，在於擺脫大國地緣爭奪，以獨立自主精神，秉持共同、綜合、合作、可持續的安全觀，透過包容和解方式，構建兼顧各方合理關切的地區安全框架。敘利亞應堅持“敘人主導、敘人所有”原則，持續推進包容性政治過渡，堅決打擊恐怖主義。戈蘭高地是國際社會公認的敘利亞被佔領土，以色列應儘早完全撤出。中方呼籲葉門各方展現政治決斷，推動重啟和平程序，爭取早日實現和解。

四是堅持標本兼治，以發展振興促進長治久安。國際社會要持續深化同中東國家互利合作，結合中東國家不同資源稟賦，助力中東實現可持續發展。要幫助衝突後國家開展重建，支援人道機構開展援助行動，推動早日恢復經濟、改善民生，為培育和平的種子創造有利土壤。必須旗幟鮮明反對保護主義和單邊行徑，堅決拒絕任何超出安理會決議範疇的非法單邊制裁。霍爾木茲海峽航道受阻衝擊全球產供鏈暢通、國際貿易秩序和能源供應穩定，當事方應回應國際社會呼聲，採取積極行動，儘快推動國際通行海峽恢復正常通航。

---

【China International Capital Co: Energy shock persists; Fed to neither cut nor hike this year】China International Capital Co says US inflation remains driven mainly by structural factors such as energy shocks; cyclical inflation is not yet evident. Rising AI-driven capex and improving employment pose upside aggregate-demand risks. Baseline: the Fed will neither cut nor raise rates this year and is expected to keep a hawkish stance. After Wosh takes office his priority will be rebuilding policy credibility; he is more likely to demonstrate resolve by reinforcing expectations for balance-sheet reduction (QT) rather than signaling rate hikes. A 'QT first, delayed cuts' outcome cannot be ruled out and would continue to pressure liquidity-dependent assets and those that benefited from excess dollar liquidity or are misaligned with his policy stance.

The protracted conflict and the ongoing near-total closure of the Strait of Hormuz disrupted energy flows from the Persian Gulf, raising concerns about resurgent inflation and potential central bank interest rate hikes. Traders modestly pared expectations for Federal Reserve rate hikes this year, although a quarter-point increase in December remains fully priced.

### 2026-06-11 07:38:55  #觀點 #國際

【中信建投：兩點因素支撐後續出口延續強勢】中信建投指出，5月出口同比增19.4%，單月出口規模創歷史新高。出口大超預期的核心驅動力是“價漲量穩”。 在高油價推動全球通脹預期再定價的背景下，中國出口正逐步從“以量換價”向“以價補量”切換。（1）價增量穩的是AI產業鏈；（2）價增量減的是稀土、手機、成品油、肥料；（3）量價齊升的是鋁材；（4）量價跌幅有所收窄的是鞋靴和箱包等勞動密集型產品；（5）以價換量的是汽車。中信建投維持後續出口延續強勢的判斷，有兩點支撐因素：一是中國依託能源成本優勢，正在承接更多“被動替代式”轉移訂單，出口產品“價漲量穩”的趨勢有望進一步擴散，出口企業利潤率與出口額或迎來同步改善。二是後續若中美達成對等降稅，鞋帽、家居等非敏感商品將受益。

Brent Jumps as US Hits Iran for Second Day — Brent climbed toward $96 per barrel on Thursday, extending gains from the previous session as the US military launched strikes against Iran for the second day, raising fears that peace efforts could collapse and the conflict disrupting global markets could drag on. President Donald Trump accused Tehran of stalling negotiations over an interim peace deal, while Tehran said it would stand firm against any threat. Earlier this week, the US launched what it described as “self-defense strikes” in response to the downing of an American helicopter, with Iran retaliating by targeting US military facilities in Bahrain, Jordan, and Kuwait. The near-total closure of the Strait of Hormuz continued to tighten global energy supplies, although there were indications that limited volumes of oil were still leaving the Persian Gulf. Meanwhile, EIA data showed US crude inventories dropped by 7.2 million barrels last week, marking a seventh consecutive weekly decline.

### 2026-06-11 07:20:51  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #RICS House Price Balance #Importance 1

UK Housing Market Remains Subdued — The RICS UK Residential Market Survey showed that the house price balance remained at -35% in May 2026, unchanged from a slightly upwardly revised April reading. This came in above market expectations of -31% and still marked the weakest level since November 2023, despite emerging signs of stabilization. New buyer enquiries steadied at -34%, marking the first month since January in which demand did not deteriorate further, while the agreed sales balance was unchanged at -37%, suggesting the recent slowdown in transactions may be losing momentum rather than accelerating. Regionally, the South East and East Anglia continued to experience greater downward pressure on prices, while Northern Ireland remained a notable outperformer with prices still rising firmly. Looking ahead, the near-term house price expectations index fell to a net balance of -45% in May at the UK-wide level, down sharply from -39%, pointing to further softness over the coming months.

【新發規模創新高 低波產品迎熱潮】低利率背景下，為有效承接銀行存款等穩健資金的進階投資需求，公募行業持續加大對於中低波動風格“固收+”產品的開拓，尤其是混合債券型二級基金以及偏債混合型FOF成為產品佈局的重中之重。截至6月10日，這兩類“固收+”產品今年以來合計新發規模超1890億元。不到半年時間，就已突破2025年全年水平。其中，主打多元資產配置的偏債混合型FOF新發規模突破千億元，新品承接的資金容量較此前出現明顯提升。尤其在近期風險資產波動明顯加劇的趨勢下，權益資產投資佔比低於15%甚至低於10%的低波類“固收+”產品，已陸續成為各家公募機構爭相佈局的重點方向。業內人士表示，隨著風險資產持續上漲，其投資價效比相比固收資產較此前有所回落，低波策略在當前的市場環境下優勢得到進一步凸顯。（中國證券報）

【歐盟與韓國簽署數字貿易協議，深化雙邊經濟關係】歐盟與韓國週三簽署了一項數字貿易協定，旨在提升數字交易便利性，並進一步加強雙方的經濟聯絡。這項數字貿易協議以及峰會上達成的其他合作內容，例如交換機密安全資訊和旅客姓名記錄，是雙方在當前地緣政治不確定性加劇的背景下推動建立新聯盟並深化現有夥伴關係的一部分。該數字貿易協議是對2011年生效的自由貿易協定的補充，旨在促進跨境資料流動，承認電子合同和電子簽名，並制定消費者保護規則。該協議還將降低企業成本，促進服務貿易。歐盟是韓國第三大商品貿易伙伴，韓國則是歐盟第八大貿易伙伴。

South Korea's Jobless Rate Holds at 2.8% — South Korea’s seasonally adjusted unemployment rate stood at 2.8% in May 2026, unchanged from April. The economy lost 40,000 jobs during the month, the first decline in employment in 17 months, amid economic uncertainty, bringing total employment to 29.120 million. A decline in employment came amid persistent weakness in mining & manufacturing and construction. Employment also dropped in agriculture, forestry & fishing, as well as wholesale & retail trade and accommodation & food services. Meanwhile, gains were recorded in electricity, transport, communication & finance, and business, personal, and public services.

New Zealand Stocks Drop, Tracking Wall Street Lower — The NZX 50 fell 45 points, or 0.3%, to 13,208 in Thursday morning deals, erasing gains from the previous two sessions and retreating from its highest level since May 7, reached a day earlier, tracking a fall on Wall Street overnight as renewed tensions in the Middle East drove oil prices higher. US inflation data also pressured sentiment, as inflation accelerated to its highest level in three years in May, reinforcing expectations that the Fed will hold interest rates steady at its upcoming meeting. Investors remained wary ahead of the release of New Zealand's business PMI for May, due on Friday, after the index recorded its slowest growth in seven months in April. Most sectors traded in the red, including financials, healthcare, and utility stocks. 【SpaceX據悉獲得三大債券評級機構的投資級評級】據知情人士透露，埃隆·馬斯克的SpaceX告訴投資者，該公司已從三家主要債券評級機構獲得了投資級評級，這可能有助於SpaceX在IPO後繼續融資時降低成本。SpaceX預計將透過首次公開募股(IPO)籌資約750億美元。該公司勢將在週四定價，並於次日開始交易。債券市場人士已開始關注Space的下一步動作，尤其是進入投資級債券市場融資的可能性。CreditSights分析師本週表示，預計SpaceX將在“IPO後不久”發行債券。根據提交給美國證券交易委員會的招股說明書，截至3月31日，SpaceX擁有291億美元長期債務，其中大部分為一筆將於2027年9月到期的200億美元過橋貸款。目前尚不清楚SpaceX具體獲得的是哪類投資級評級，例如是否僅適用於有擔保債務。知情人士表示，穆迪評級、惠譽評級和標普全球評級均給予SpaceX投資級評級。

### 2026-06-11 05:12:33  #Trading Economics #Economy #El Salvador #Producer Prices Change #Importance 0

El Salvador Producer Prices Rise in May — Producer prices in El Salvador rose by 2.78% year-on-year in May 2026, accelerating from a 1.59% increase in the previous month. The pickup was largely driven by higher costs for electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning (10.34% vs 1.70% in April), activities of administrative and support services (5.20% vs 4.62%), healthcare and social assistance (4.44%, unchanged), transport and storage (3.22% vs 3.26%), and accommodation and food services (2.59% vs 2.20%). Producer prices continued to decline for information and telecommunications (-0.07%, unchanged), while water supply, sewerage, waste management and remediation activities also remained in negative territory (-0.15% vs 0.01%). Other notable increases were recorded in real estate activities (1.36% vs 0.81%) and other service activities (3.80% vs 2.23%).

### 2026-06-11 05:07:58  #Trading Economics #Markets #Brazil #Stock Market #Importance 0

Ibovespa Falls to Lowest Since January — The Ibovespa fell 0.7% to close at 168,619 on Wednesday, extending losses to its lowest level since January as escalating geopolitical tensions fueled risk aversion. US President Donald Trump warned that Washington would strike Iran “with great force” if no peace agreement is reached after Tehran downed a US helicopter near the Strait of Hormuz, raising concerns over a broader conflict. Higher crude prices pushed bond yields up and reinforced expectations of a more hawkish BCB. Market pricing now suggests a pause in the central bank's easing cycle at next week's meeting, with investors increasingly betting that the next move in interest rates will be upward. Banks traded lower, with Bradesco down 1%. WEG extended losses, falling 2.2% after reporting weaker-than-expected earnings on Tuesday. Other notable laggards included Vale (-1%), Rede D'Or (-2.5%), and Embraer (-4.2%). On the upside, Petrobras gained 1.2% on higher oil prices.

Paraguay Posts Trade Surplus in May — Paraguay’s trade balance swung to a $98.1 million surplus in May 2026 from a $201.3 million deficit in the same month a year earlier. Exports rose 22.6% year-on-year to $1.70 billion, driven mainly by a 58.4% increase in primary goods shipments. Exports of industrial manufactures advanced 33.4%, while exports of agricultural manufactures fell 7.5%. Shipments of fuels and energy declined 19.6%. Meanwhile, imports increased 0.8% to $1.60 billion. Purchases of consumer goods rose 6.4%, while imports of intermediate goods increased 20.4%. In contrast, imports of capital goods fell 13.7%.

【Sources: WHITE HOUSE to meet defense contractors on ramping up production】Sources say the Trump administration plans to meet as early as next week at the WHITE HOUSE with executives from the largest U.S. defense contractors to discuss accelerating weapons production after U.S. strikes on Iran and other recent military actions depleted supplies. The meeting would be the WHITE HOUSE's second CEO-level convening on boosting weapons output; a March session included CEOs from BAE Systems, Lockheed Martin and Boeing and the defense secretary. ### 2026-06-11 03:24:30  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #WTI #Impact bearish #Brent

【40 mln barrels left under US SPR loan plan】The US Department of Energy said on Wednesday it will seek to lend up to 40 mln barrels from the Strategic Petroleum Reserve to energy firms to help lower fuel prices. The loans are part of a previously announced 172 mln-barrel SPR release; about 133 mln barrels have been lent so far. In March, after the US and Israel launched a war on Iran on Feb. 28, the US and roughly 30 IEA members agreed to jointly release about 400 mln barrels to help stabilize the oil market. US SPR stocks stood at 349.2 mln barrels, the lowest since Aug 2023. Borrowers must return an equivalent volume and pay up to a 24% premium in additional oil.

### 2026-06-11 03:17:40  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Government Budget Value #Importance 1

US Budget Deficit Narrows in May — The US government recorded a $293 billion budget deficit in May 2026, compared with a $316 billion gap a year earlier, but wider than forecasts of a $275 billion shortfall. Government outlays declined to $628 billion from $687 billion. The largest spending categories were Social Security ($140 billion), net interest payments ($107 billion), Medicare ($87 billion), and health programs ($82 billion). Meanwhile, receipts fell to $335 billion from $371 billion. The main sources of revenue were social insurance and retirement contributions ($157 billion), individual income taxes ($152 billion), and corporate income taxes ($11 billion). In the first eight months of fiscal year 2026, the federal budget deficit totaled $1.25 trillion.

### 2026-06-11 02:55:40  #MKT News #Market Themes #AI Revolution

【ANTHROPIC CEO says government should be able to block deployment of new AI models】ANTHROPIC CEO Dario Amodei said in a long post Wednesday that governments should have the authority to block deployment of new AI models that pose specific or "unacceptable" risks. He called for mandatory third-party testing to assess models' risks across multiple domains and compared the current phase of AI development to cars, planes and drugs — critical to the economy but capable of causing mass harm if misdesigned or misused. Amodei's remarks are his strongest to date in favor of tighter AI regulation. Brent Rises Above $93 — Brent extended gains and rose more than 2% to $93 a barrel on Wednesday as traders weighed escalating tensions in the Middle East. President Trump vowed to attack Iran very hard again and said that Iran had taken too long to negotiate a deal and would now “have to pay the price”. The comments follow Iranian attacks on several Gulf nations, including Bahrain, Jordan, and Kuwait, after US “self-defense strikes” in response to the downing of an American helicopter. Traffic through the Strait of Hormuz remains significantly disrupted, with Iran blocking most shipping through the key waterway, while the US has imposed its own restrictions on Iranian ports. However, US Energy Secretary Wright said vessel traffic in the Gulf and oil exports through the Strait are rising despite ongoing disruptions. Meanwhile, EIA data showed US crude inventories fell by 7.228 million barrels last week, a seventh consecutive weekly decline and surpassing expectations for a 4 million barrel draw.

【Axios: Trump added two demands to Iran, delaying preliminary deal】Axios reports former President Trump likely could have struck a preliminary deal with Iran in late May if he had accepted terms negotiated by his team, but after a May 29 White House Situation Room meeting he added two conditions that delayed agreement. The new demands were that Iran begin downblending its enriched-uranium stockpile within 60 days and pledge not to levy transit fees on vessels transiting the Strait of Hormuz. Trump was willing to accept the downblending to occur inside Iran under IAEA supervision. Iran’s foreign minister told mediators and U.S. officials he needed four to five days to respond, but the pause extended to nearly two weeks. During the delay, Iran sought partial release of frozen assets before implementing obligations, while Trump insisted obligations be met first; U.S. domestic criticism of Trump’s approach also intensified.

### 2026-06-11 00:32:00  #Trading Economics #Economy #Russia #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Russia Inflation Rate at Near 3-Year Low — Russia's headline annual inflation rate fell for the second month to 5.3% in May 2026, down from 5.6% in April and below market estimates of 5.4%. This was the lowest reading since August 2023, but it remains well above the CBR’s target of 4%. Food price growth slowed to 2.9%, compared to 4% in April, as fruit and vegetable prices continued to decline (-10.4% vs -6.6%). Meanwhile, prices advanced faster for services (10.1% vs 9.9%) and non-food products (4.2% vs 3.9%). The core rate edged down to 4.9% in May from 5% in the previous month. On a monthly basis, the CPI rose by 0.2%, after a 0.1% increase in the prior month, in line with forecasts.

【ASML to cut fewer jobs than planned after union talks】ASML (ASML.O) said after negotiations with Dutch unions it will cut fewer jobs than previously planned and that dismissals will not take effect until after May 1 next year. A union spokesperson said employees will be notified next month after consultation with the works council; affected staff have until May 2027 to secure new roles and may receive up to €400,000 in compensation. ASML said the final headcount will be set once the process is complete and the company aims to reduce the overall scale of the cuts; in January it had announced plans to cut about 1,700 roles, roughly 4% of its workforce.

FTSE MIB Slides Amid Rising Geopolitical Risks — The FTSE MIB fell 0.5% to close at 50,029 on Wednesday, weighed down by escalating tensions in the Middle East. Iran launched attacks against several Gulf nations after US retaliatory strikes following the downing of an American helicopter. Oil prices rose, renewing inflation fears and pushing bond yields higher. Financials traded mixed, with UniCredit down 1.3% and Intesa Sanpaolo losing 0.9%, while Generali gained 0.7% and Banco BPM rose 3.7%. Earlier this week, Intesa Sanpaolo, together with Unipol (+1.5%), launched a public exchange offer for MPS (-0.9%), a move that will also affect affiliate BPER (+0.2%). Banco BPM had proposed a friendly merger with MPS on Sunday. Luxury shares weakened amid higher risk aversion, with Ferrari down 1.5% and Moncler losing 0.9%. In contrast, Eni gained 1.6% on rising oil prices.

CAC 40 Falls on Middle East Tensions Escalation — The CAC 40 fell 0.5% to close at 8,162 on Wednesday, weighed down by escalating tensions in the Middle East. Iran launched attacks against several Gulf nations after US retaliatory strikes following the downing of an American helicopter. Oil prices rose, renewing inflation fears and pushing bond yields higher. Financials traded mixed, with BNP Paribas down 1.5% and Societe Generale losing 2.3%, while AXA gained 0.9%. Luxury shares weighed on the index, with LVMH falling 0.9% and Hermes shedding 2.8%. Safran lost 1.4%. STMicroelectronics erased earlier gains despite a Bank of America upgrade to "buy", as enthusiasm around AI faded. Sanofi fell 1.3% after halting a late-stage trial for an experimental autoimmune treatment. Investors also awaited Thursday's ECB meeting, with markets expecting a rate hike.

UK Stocks Outperform — London’s FTSE 100 reversed early losses to close around 0.3% higher at 10,255 on Wednesday, recovering from the previous session’s decline and outperforming major European peers. The main support came from Tritax Big Box REIT, which jumped 4.9% after confirming it had secured a successful appeal for its £360m Heathrow data centre project, boosting sentiment across property-linked stocks. Broader gains were supported by Entain, insurer Admiral Group and grocer Tesco. Among heavyweights, Shell and BP provided key uplift, alongside defensives British American Tobacco, Unilever, GSK and Compass Group, all rising between 1%-2%. On the downside, miners such as Endeavour Mining (-4.1%), Fresnillo (-1.7%) and Glencore (-0.9%) weakened. UK lenders faced pressure too, with HSBC down 1.3% and Standard Chartered falling 1.1%, following a JP Morgan note on the impact of new Chinese regulations.

### 2026-06-10 23:49:18  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Natural Gas Prices Edge Up — US natural gas prices edged up to $3.19 per MMBtu, supported by forecasts for above-normal temperatures in the second half of June, which are expected to boost cooling demand. However, prices remain below the recent highs seen earlier in the month. According to LSEG, average gas production in the US Lower 48 has eased to 108.8 billion cubic feet per day in June, down from 109.7 bcfd in May. While the decline in output has helped narrow the storage surplus, inventories remain about 5% above the five-year seasonal average, pointing to broadly comfortable supply conditions heading into the summer. Meanwhile, net flows to major LNG export terminals have fallen to 16.3 bcfd so far in June from 17.1 bcfd in May, as seasonal maintenance at facilities including Golden Pass and Freeport LNG in Texas continues to weigh on export volumes.

Mozambique Inflation Rate Hits 2023-Peak — The annual inflation rate in Mozambique picked up to 7.22% in May 2026, accelerating from 4.41% in the prior month and marking the steepest rise since May 2023. The increase was largely driven by a fuel shock, following supply disruptions in April tied to geopolitical tensions in the Middle East, which led to sharp adjustments in diesel and gasoline prices earlier in May. On a monthly basis, the CPI climbed by 2.32% in May, the steepest increase since February 2019, after a 0.63% rise in the prior month.

### 2026-06-10 23:24:00  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Canada 10-Year Yield Steady After BoC Rate Hold — Canada’s 10-year government bond yield held steady near 3.5% after the Bank of Canada left its benchmark interest rate unchanged at 2.25%, in line with expectations. The central bank noted that uncertainty remains elevated amid escalating tensions in the Middle East and new US tariff proposals, while reiterating that it stands ready to act if necessary and will not allow higher energy prices to feed persistent inflation. Markets continue to price in a 25bps rate hike by December. However, the 10-year yield remains below its May highs, tracking a decline in US Treasury yields as investors speculate that a potential US–Iran agreement could help ease geopolitical tensions.

Canadian Dollar Strengthens After BoC Holds Rates — The Canadian dollar strengthened slightly to around 1.39 per US dollar after the Bank of Canada left its key interest rate unchanged at 2.25%, as widely expected. The central bank noted that uncertainty remains elevated amid escalating tensions in the Middle East and new US tariff proposals, but reiterated that it stands ready to act if needed and will not allow higher energy prices to feed sustained inflation. ### 2026-06-10 22:45:31  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #WTI #Impact bullish #Brent

【Reuters survey: OPEC crude output at lowest since 2000; Iran exports hit six-year low】A Reuters survey showed OPEC crude output fell 1.06 mln bpd in May to 16.13 mln bpd, the lowest monthly level since 2000 and well below 2020 pandemic lows. The decline reflects US naval blockade measures affecting Iranian exports since April 13 and an effective closure of the Strait of Hormuz that sharply cut other Gulf flows. Iran's crude and condensate exports fell to at least a six-year low. Data exclude the UAE, which left OPEC on May 1. Sources said Iraq boosted shipments to meet higher domestic demand, Saudi output fell further, while Venezuela and Nigeria increased production.

US Crude Inventories Drop for 7th Week — US crude inventories fell by 7.228 million barrels in the week ended June 5, 2026, marking the seventh consecutive weekly decline and surpassing expectations for a 4 million barrel draw. Stocks at the Cushing, Oklahoma hub also decreased by 801,000 barrels, extending their decline for a seventh week. Refinery activity increased, with crude runs rising by 81,000 barrels per day. In contrast, gasoline inventories rose by 186,000 barrels, defying forecasts for a 0.5 million barrel decline, while distillate stocks, including diesel and heating oil, increased by 200,000 barrels, compared with expectations for a 0.5 million barrel draw. Net US crude imports rose by 525,000 barrels per day over the week.

### 2026-06-10 22:35:43  #央行 #國際

【中國人民銀行與國際清算銀行共同主辦“應對全球金融不確定性——金融市場發展、投資策略與國際貨幣體系”高階別會議】2026年6月10日，中國人民銀行與國際清算銀行在上海共同主辦“應對全球金融不確定性——金融市場發展、投資策略與國際貨幣體系”高階別會議。中國人民銀行行長潘功勝與國際清算銀行總經理巴勃羅·埃爾南德斯·德科斯（Pablo Hernández de Cos)共同出席並主持會議，中國人民銀行副行長宣昌能出席會議。來自巴西、阿根廷、印度尼西亞、沙烏地阿拉伯、卡達、法國、德國、瑞士、英國等全球約30個國家和地區的中央銀行行長或高階別代表參會。會上，各方就全球宏觀經濟形勢與金融市場發展、外匯市場走勢與機構投資者資產配置策略、亞太地區金融市場機遇與新興市場本幣債券配置、全球外匯儲備管理趨勢與國際貨幣體系多元化等議題交換了意見。潘功勝表示，近年來，中國金融市場持續穩定發展，對外開放成效顯著。在全球經濟面臨地緣衝突和保護主義等多重挑戰、國際貨幣體系多元化程序深化、全球金融市場波動加劇的背景下，中國金融市場的深度與廣度為境外機構投資者提供了重要的多元化配置機遇。國際清算銀行總經理德科斯表示，當前全球經濟金融環境面臨地緣政治緊張、經濟碎片化、債務水平高企與技術變革等多重挑戰，不確定性顯著上升，非銀行金融機構的快速擴張也對金融穩定提出新的要求。在此背景下，國際合作對於維護全球金融體系的穩定至關重要。(央行)

【150億資金出逃，基金經理：支撐黃金中長期價值的核心支柱依然穩固】曾經被稱作“避險之王”的黃金，如今持續上演回撤行情，此輪調整是短期技術性回撥，還是黃金牛市徹底終結的訊號？“本輪黃金股的急跌是典型的脈衝式擾動，而非基本面邏輯的結構性反轉。”黃金股ETF永贏基金經理劉庭宇對第一財經表示，在市場恐慌情緒蔓延之際，支撐黃金中長期價值的核心支柱依然穩固，板塊在經歷快速殺跌後，投資價效比反而更加凸顯。行情持續走弱的背景下，市場資金正加速從黃金類ETF撤離。據統計，截至6月9日，超七成黃金類ETF近一個月出現資金淨流出，累計資金“失血”148.59億元。其中，規模最大的華安黃金ETF資金出逃最為明顯，同期淨流出金額近79億元，產品規模也從千億級別回落至981.92億元，頭部產品資金撤退效應顯著。對於黃金股與黃金商品走勢的差異，一位量化投研人士解釋稱，黃金股ETF投資標的為黃金礦業公司股票，相較於黃金價格本身，黃金股同時具備黃金價格與企業盈利增長的雙重槓桿效應，潛在彈性更高。（第一財經）

TSX Edges Lower Amid Middle East Tensions — The S&P/TSX Composite Index edged lower to trade below 34,500 on Wednesday, reflecting a broader risk-off tone as tensions in the Middle East escalated. The US struck targets in Iran in retaliation for an attack on a helicopter near the Strait of Hormuz, while Iran responded with strikes on US bases in Kuwait and Bahrain. Meanwhile, the Bank of Canada held interest rates steady as expected, citing mixed domestic economic signals, though markets continue to expect a 25bps rate hike by year-end. Financial stocks were broadly weaker, with Brookfield slipping nearly 1%. Technology shares also remained under pressure amid fading enthusiasm around AI, with Shopify down almost 2%. Mining stocks declined as gold prices eased, with Agnico Eagle and Wheaton Precious Metals losing around 2% and Barrick Gold falling 1.5%. In contrast, energy stocks outperformed, supported by higher crude prices. Canadian Natural and Suncor each gained about 1%, while Cenovus advanced more than 1.5%.

【快速納入指數引爭議 業內警示SpaceX股價波動傳導風險】如今，指數機構與基金管理人因SpaceX這場充滿爭議的IPO被捆綁在一起。該企業目前仍處於大幅虧損狀態，且對股東設定了嚴格的治理許可權限制。按照常規IPO走勢，新股上市數月內往往容易破發，一旦SpaceX股價下跌，負面影響或將蔓延至整個指數成分股板塊。 業內人士還擔憂，本次IPO體量過於龐大，本身就潛藏諸多風險。曾參與Facebook、ARM上市諮詢工作的企業上市諮詢機構Issuer Network執行長希利直言，大幅縮短指數納入週期的做法，一旦SpaceX或其他走快速通道的新股上市後下跌，很可能拖累指數內其他個股。 “規則壓縮到如此極限，無異於自找麻煩，風險很高。” 與此同時，SpaceX向散戶傾斜大量股份，而散戶交易行為相比機構更難預判，股價劇烈波動的風險隨之上升，也可能引發市場整體情緒轉差。

【國際機構看好中國機器人產業】今年以來，中國經濟的科技屬性持續增強，海內外權威機構釋出的研究報告紛紛認為，創新動能已成為吸引外資持續深耕中國的核心驅動力。新加坡星展集團首席經濟學家泰穆爾·拜格表示：“過去數十年，中國在教育研發、產業投入上打下了堅實基礎，這將推動高科技、綠色科技與服務業持續創造高附加值，成為經濟增長的重要來源。過去數十年的持續投入，將在未來逐步釋放巨大紅利，中國經濟的發展前景非常值得期待。”美國韋德布什證券公司全球科技研究主管稱：“觀察中國的大型科技公司，可以看到相關需求正在不斷湧現，中國人工智慧產業下一階段增長才剛剛開始。未來增長點在機器人領域。在我看來，人形機器人將是中國市場釋放大量需求的一個重要方向。”目前，高盛、摩根大通、瑞銀、花旗等多家國際頂級投行，均維持對中國A股的超配、增持或買入評級。 (央視財經)

Baltic Dry Index Losing Run Continues — The Baltic Exchange's dry bulk freight index, which monitors rates for ships carrying dry bulk commodities, extended its decline into a ninth consecutive session on Wednesday, falling about 1.7% to a fresh low since May 1st at 2,771 points. The capesize index, which typically transports 150,000-ton cargoes including iron ore and coal, dropped about 3.2% to 4,301 points. Conversely, the panamax index, which tracks vessels carrying around 60,000 to 70,000 tons of coal or grain, went up by 0.3% to 2,211 points; and the supramax index rose by 0.3% to 1,618 points.

### 2026-06-10 21:49:41  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Interest Rate #Importance 3

BoC Leaves Rates Steady, Stands Ready to Raise if Needed — The Bank of Canada left the target for its benchmark overnight rate steady at 2.25% for a fifth consecutive meeting in June 2026, in line with expectations. Policymakers said that so far, there has been limited evidence of broad-based pass-through of higher energy prices to other consumer prices. However, the central bank is continuing to look through the war’s near-term impact on inflation and will not let higher energy prices become persistent inflation, standing ready to respond as needed. Inflation in Canada rose to 2.8% in April mostly due to energy prices, but the core rate moved down to 2.1%. The central bank expects inflation to hover around 3% before gradually easing towards the 2% target. Policymakers also noted that economic activity in Canada has been weak and uncertainty about US trade policy persists. The Bank Rate and the deposit rate were also kept at 2.5% and 2.20% respectively.

### 2026-06-10 21:43:58  #Trading Economics #Markets #Brazil #Currency #Importance 1

Brazilian Real Holds Near Two-Month Low — The Brazilian real traded near 5.19 per USD in June, remaining close to its weakest level in two months as broad dollar strength prevailed amid heightened tensions in the Middle East. Concerns intensified after a US helicopter was shot down near the Strait of Hormuz, prompting retaliatory US strikes and boosting demand for safe-haven assets. Meanwhile, elevated US inflation and a tight labor market reinforced expectations of a hawkish Federal Reserve. In Brazil, investors also monitored a poll showing President Lula widening his lead over Senator Flávio Bolsonaro. Economic data remained resilient, with GDP expanding 1.1% in the first quarter, above expectations, while annual inflation accelerated to 4.39% in April. Market pricing now suggests a pause in the central bank's easing cycle at next week's meeting, with investors increasingly betting that the next move in interest rates will be upward.

US Stocks Lower as Tech Sector Drags — US stocks were lower on Wednesday, with the S&P 500 down 0.6%, the Nasdaq falling 0.8%, and the Dow Jones losing 280 points, as a renewed tech selloff weighed on sentiment amid lingering concerns over elevated AI-related valuations. Nvidia (-1.4%), Broadcom (-3.9%), and Micron Technology (-3.5%) were among the steepest decliners, while Oracle slipped 2.4% ahead of its earnings release after the closing bell. The industrial sector also weakened, although energy stocks outperformed as oil prices rose amid continued strikes between Iran and the US. Meanwhile, the latest CPI report showed US annual inflation accelerating to 4.2% in May, in line with expectations, while core inflation rose less than anticipated on a monthly basis, increasing 0.2%. The data offered some reassurance that the energy-driven shock has not yet meaningfully spilled over into broader price pressures. Still, markets continue to fully price in a 25-basis-point rate hike in December.

### 2026-06-10 21:35:43  #宏觀 #公司

【國家資料局：劉烈宏一行在京調研資料市場建設情況】6月2日，國家資料局黨組書記、局長劉烈宏帶隊在北京市開展資料市場建設專題調研。調研組先後到首都醫科大學附屬北京友誼醫院、北京高階資料標註基地、北京國際大資料交易所、北京資料集團、醫渡科技公司開展實地調研，在友誼醫院聽取了北京市衛生健康委、同仁醫院、胸科醫院、安貞醫院等單位的介紹，深入瞭解資料資源開發利用進展情況。調研期間，北京市政務服務和資料管理局等有關單位介紹了建設國家資料要素綜合試驗區、推進資料要素市場化配置改革的有關情況。在醫療健康領域，北京市資料管理部門和衛生健康部門協同推進，建設了統一的醫學影像雲平臺，推進檢查檢驗資訊互通互認，北京市二級及以上醫療機構的影像共享壁壘已全面打通，並正推動影像雲平臺接入所有社群衛生服務機構，實現電子膠片服務全域覆蓋，打破了醫療檢查資料“孤島”，改變了患者帶著膠片跑醫院的傳統就醫模式；開展醫療健康行業高質量資料集建設，在以高質量醫療健康資料賦能智慧診療與藥企研發等方面取得積極進展。在培育資料流通服務機構方面，北京國際大資料交易所加快轉型，在制度建設和生態培育方面持續用力，北京資料集團創新發展模式，在公共資料資源開發利用和行業高質量資料集建設方面探索創新，取得積極進展。關於下一步工作，大家一致表示，要深入貫徹落實黨中央、國務院關於推進資料要素市場化配置改革有關決策部署，更大力度推進資料要素綜合試驗區建設，深化醫療健康資料資源開發利用，更好地釋放資料要素價值；要加快培育資料流通服務機構，充分發揮資料集團的作用，促進公共資料資源開發利用，加快建設開放共享安全的全國一體化資料市場。

【Office of State Council Work Safety Committee and three ministries summon China Communications Construction Group and PowerChina】On June 10 the Office of the State Council Work Safety Committee, the Ministry of Housing and Urban‑Rural Development, the Ministry of Transport and the State‑owned Assets Supervision and Administration Commission (SASAC) jointly summoned the chairmen of China Communications Construction Group and PowerChina and senior heads of certain second‑tier subsidiaries. Regulators told the firms to absorb lessons from recent disaster accidents, raise political priority on safety and put production safety first; implement the accident‑investigation "two closed‑loop" requirements; pursue problem‑oriented rectification and crack down on falsification and shoddy workmanship. They ordered stricter subcontracting controls to end 'contracting instead of management', deeper hidden‑hazard inspections and comprehensive upgrades to risk controls. With extreme weather expected during the flood season, the groups were instructed to increase vigilance and take stronger, more decisive measures to secure safety.

US Energy Inflation Quickens in May — US energy inflation accelerated further to 23.5% year-over-year in May 2026, up from 17.9% in April and marking the sharpest annual increase since August 2022. The rise was driven by a 40.6% jump in energy commodities amid the Middle East conflict and ongoing disruptions in the Strait of Hormuz, with gasoline prices increasing 40.5% and fuel oil prices surging 58.9%. On a monthly basis, energy prices advanced by 3.9%, after a 3.8% surge in the preceding month.

US CPI Rises 0.5% MoM in May — The CPI in the US rose 0.5% month-over-month in May 2026, slowing from a 0.6% rise in April and matching forecasts. Energy prices increased 3.9%, after rising 3.8% in April and 10.9% in March. The energy index accounted for over 60% of the monthly increase, amid the energy shock caused by the conflict with Iran. Gasoline soared 7% and fuel oil went up 3.8%. Meanwhile, prices also increased for shelter (0.3%) and food (0.2%). Other gains were seen in prices for communication, airline fares, medical care, personal care, and recreation. Conversely, the indexes for motor vehicle insurance, household furnishings and operations, and new vehicles decreased.

【知情人士：SpaceX IPO獲中東基金數十億美元認購】多位知情人士透露，海灣主權財富基金已大手筆認購SpaceX IPO股份，總額達數十億美元，這也再度印證中東希望持續主導全球人工智慧產業資金供給的宏大布局。 訊息人士稱，沙特公共投資基金（PIF）、科威特投資局（KIA）分別遞交了10億—50億美元的認購單；管理5800億美元資產的卡達投資局（QIA）大機率也會大手筆參投。 知情人士表示，中東本土機構本就是馬斯克這家火箭、衛星、AI企業的重要股東；按照公司1.8萬億美元的目標估值，這些持倉賬面浮盈豐厚。目前暫不清楚各大基金此番新增認購中，有多少資金是用來對沖上市後的股權稀釋。 不止中東，全球各大機構都在爭搶份額，整體認購需求已經超額。彭博此前報道，部分機構申報認購金額超100億美元，不過最終實際配售額度大機率會縮水。 沙特公共投資基金、卡達投資局發言人拒絕置評，科威特投資局尚未回應採訪問詢。（環球市場播報）

【2026上海國際碳中和博覽會開幕，龔正包鋼劉振民出席並致辭】以“零碳變革，要素賦能”為主題的2026上海國際碳中和技術、產品與成果博覽會今天（6月10日）在上海新國際博覽中心開幕。上海市委副書記、市長龔正，內蒙古自治區黨委副書記、主席包鋼，中國氣候變化事務特使劉振民出席開幕式並致辭。龔正在致辭中指出，綠色低碳轉型是時代潮流、大勢所趨，是厚植高質量發展綠色底色的內在要求。上海將全面貫徹落實習近平生態文明思想，以碳達峰碳中和為牽引，協同推進降碳、減汙、擴綠、增長，加快形成綠色生產方式和生活方式，推動經濟社會發展全面綠色低碳轉型。上海國際碳博會自2023年舉辦以來，已成為展示綠色低碳轉型成果、促進綠色低碳交流的重要平臺。我們願與各方一道，共赴綠色之約、共創美好未來，攜手共建清潔、美麗、可持續的世界。（上海釋出）

【US CPI preview: core inflation seen mild, could ease market rate‑hike fears】Economists Durie and Wong say May headline US CPI is likely to be strong, but core inflation—more relevant for the Fed rate outlook—should remain mild, with the month-on-month increase consistent with a 2% target. Supply shocks have pushed up airfares, appliances and electronics, while disinflation in non-essential services suggests consumers are cutting discretionary spending. A muted core print would ease market concern about further Fed hikes after May's stronger-than-expected nonfarm payrolls. The economists still expect a 25bps Fed cut in Q4.

### 2026-06-10 20:09:22  #公司 #市場

【養雞板塊集體拉昇 頭部雞苗上市公司回應】養雞板塊上市公司股價6月10日集體拉昇。某雞苗頭部上市公司回應稱，當前，下游養殖戶對雞苗的補欄逐步恢復正常。預計雞蛋供應和庫存量也會逐步恢復正常。“雞苗長到160天左右就可以產蛋，而雞的自然壽命有7到8年，養殖戶不可能等雞那麼老才補欄。正常情況下，一批蛋雞隻要觸及盈虧平衡點，就要淘汰、補欄。去年需要淘汰的老母雞和雞蛋價格都在低位，所以很多養殖戶對雞苗的補欄需求有所下滑，這也直接導致了當前市場老年雞的數量佔比較高。老年雞的產蛋效率較低，導致一些地區雞蛋供應出現緊平衡，推高了雞蛋價格。”上述雞苗上市公司相關負責人稱，“冷鏈儲存技術提升後，雞蛋的儲存期延長、庫存調節能力增強，也不再是單純的現貨產品。所以不排除部分地區對雞蛋有炒作的成分。現在價格抬升後，養殖戶補欄積極性高了，有望加速處置淘汰的老母雞，換成產蛋效率高的母雞。” (中證金牛座)

### 2026-06-10 19:44:13  #宏觀

【中央網信辦召開全國網路生態治理工作會議】6月10日，中央網信辦在京召開全國網路生態治理工作會議。會議堅持以習近平新時代中國特色社會主義思想為指導，深入學習貫徹習近平文化思想、習近平總書記關於網路強國的重要思想，深入貫徹落實習近平總書記在中央政治局第二十三次集體學習時的重要講話精神，總結治理成效，分析形勢任務，部署下一階段工作。中央宣傳部副部長、中央網信辦主任、國家網信辦主任莊榮文出席會議並講話，中央網信辦副主任、國家網信辦副主任牛一兵主持會議。

會議指出，黨的十八大以來，以習近平同志為核心的黨中央高度重視網路生態治理工作。習近平總書記洞察資訊革命發展大勢，把握管網治網工作規律，圍繞加強網路生態治理作出一系列重大部署、提出一系列明確要求。2025年11月28日，習近平總書記在中央政治局第二十三次集體學習時發表重要講話，深刻闡述了加強網路生態治理的重大意義、成效經驗、目標方向、主要任務，為加強網路生態治理、推動網信事業高質量發展指明瞭前進方向、提供了根本遵循。近年來，全國網信系統和網站平臺深入貫徹落實習近平總書記重要講話、重要指示批示精神和黨中央決策部署，紮實推進網路生態治理工作，持續健全長效機制，治理能力手段更加強化，平臺主體責任進一步發揮，網路生態治理取得顯著成效，網路生態總體向上向好。

會議強調，加強網路生態治理，是服務黨和國家工作大局的必然要求，是堅定不移推進網路強國建設的重要舉措，是貫徹以人民為中心的發展思想的內在體現，是促進平臺經濟健康發展的基礎保障，要提高政治站位、深化思想認識，進一步增強做好網路生態治理工作的責任感使命感緊迫感。要明確目標任務、聚焦工作重點，持續提升網路生態治理能力水平。在強化標本兼治上下功夫，提升專項行動質效，完善長效治理機制，抓好制度措施落實。在強化基礎管理上下功夫，把好網際網路入口關，把好重要流量入口關，把好新業態新應用入口關。在強化平臺履責上下功夫，加強平臺規則治理，提升賬號管理效能，強化平臺運營管理，規範和管理未成年人使用網路。在強化技術賦能上下功夫，興利除弊發展新興技術，與時俱進豐富技術手段，前瞻佈局推動技術向善。

UK Gilt Yields Rise on Inflation Fears — UK 10-year gilt yields rose toward 4.95%, their highest since May 21, as inflation concerns intensified amid escalating Middle East tensions. Iranian Parliament Speaker Mohammad Bagher Ghalibaf also stated on Wednesday that Tehran would respond "decisively and without delay" to any aggression. Rising energy costs from the conflict have heightened inflation fears, prompting investors to price in at least a 25-basis-point rate hike by the Bank of England in September, with a strong chance of a second increase. However, dovish Monetary Policy Committee member Alan Taylor, who backed the 8-1 vote to hold rates in April, said on Monday that current interest rates are "quite restrictive" and saw no need for further tightening to control inflation.

Any signs of price-pressure diffusion would reinforce the Fed’s cautious policy stance; a mildly on‑target, subdued report would support the view that current inflation is transitory and supply‑driven rather than demand‑driven.

Brent Up to $92 — Brent rose about 2% to $92 a barrel on Wednesday as traders weighed escalating tensions in the Middle East. US President Donald Trump said Iran had taken too long to negotiate a deal and would now “have to pay the price” and that he is close to ordering strikes on Iran's plants and bridges. The comments follow Iranian attacks on several Gulf nations, including Bahrain, Jordan, and Kuwait, after US “self-defence strikes” in response to the downing of an American helicopter. Traffic through the Strait of Hormuz remains significantly disrupted, with Iran blocking most shipping through the key waterway, while the US has imposed its own restrictions on Iranian ports. However, US Energy Secretary Wright said vessel traffic in the Gulf and oil exports through the Strait are rising despite ongoing disruptions. Meanwhile, API data showed US crude inventories fell by 9.1 million barrels last week to their lowest level in four months.

【光伏龍頭股突然絕地反攻，強制效能耗能效標準或於近期出臺？】光伏板塊消沉的市場情緒驟然反轉。6月10日尾盤，晶澳科技、愛旭股份直線漲停，隆基綠能逼近漲停。這一絕地反攻頗為突然，大量資金在最後半小時集中湧入。該板塊的久違大漲疑與午後一則傳聞有關。有網傳訊息稱，“下週好像發光伏強制標準，涉及矽料、矽片、元件，按之前口徑大概能清退30%產能。”澎湃新聞向上述多家企業求證該訊息，均回應注意到有該說法，但目前無法證實其真實性。“小作文”真偽莫辨，但近來政策預期確實有所升溫。上週舉行的SNEC光儲展期間，有光伏企業負責人透露，據其瞭解，備受聚焦的三級能效強制標準最近有望出臺。據瞭解，技術優勢顯著、但苦於全行業產能出清遲緩的光伏企業均對該政策翹首以盼，這將構成業內擇優招標採購、淘汰落後產品的技術依據。“達不到標準的企業會很難。金融機構一看你沒有能力再做技改了，馬上就從金融方面給你停了。”上述負責人認為，三級能效標準應儘快出臺、持續最佳化提升。（澎湃）

【工信部公佈關於《道路機動車輛生產企業及產品公告》（第408批）和《享受車船稅減免優惠的節約能源 使用新能源汽車車型目錄》（第八十七批）擬釋出內容的公示】根據道路機動車輛生產企業及產品准入管理和《財政部 稅務總局 工業和資訊化部 交通運輸部關於節能 新能源車船享受車船稅優惠政策的通知》（財稅〔2018〕74號）、《工業和資訊化部 財政部 稅務總局關於調整享受車船稅優惠的節能 新能源汽車產品技術要求的公告》（2025年第25號）等有關要求，現將《道路機動車輛生產企業及產品公告》（第408批）和《享受車船稅減免優惠的節約能源 使用新能源汽車車型目錄》（第八十七批）擬釋出內容予以公示，請社會各界監督，如有異議，請在公示期內透過網上意見徵求系統（詳見附件連結）、傳真反饋。

本批申請新產品准入的車輛生產企業共計743戶，其中汽車生產企業564戶、摩托車生產企業179戶。以上企業申報的新產品共計2454個，其中汽車產品1926個、摩托車產品528個。申報新能源汽車產品的共有304戶企業的963個型號，其中純電動產品共223戶企業802個型號、插電式混合動力產品共66戶企業123個型號、燃料電池產品共15戶企業38個型號。

本批申請產品變更擴充套件的車輛生產企業共計1160戶，其中汽車生產企業935戶、摩托車生產企業224戶、三輪汽車生產企業1戶。以上企業申報的變更擴充套件產品共計12491個，其中汽車產品10863個、摩托車產品1621個、三輪汽車產品7個。本批有14戶汽車生產企業的19個汽車產品申請整改。

### 2026-06-10 19:12:07  #宏觀 #焦點

【國家發展改革委主任鄭柵潔主持召開經濟形勢專家座談會 圍繞當前經濟形勢和做好下一步經濟工作聽取意見建議】6月10日，國家發展改革委主任鄭柵潔主持召開經濟形勢專家座談會，與中國社科院學部委員蔡昉、賽迪研究院院長張立以及中銀證券等部分國內外券商首席經濟學家進行座談交流，圍繞分析研判當前經濟形勢、持續擴大內需、推動高水平科技自立自強和產業鏈自主可控、著力穩就業穩企業穩市場穩預期等方面，聽取與會專家的觀點看法和意見建議。座談會上，與會專家暢所欲言，詳細介紹了對當前中國經濟形勢的看法。大家認為，今年以來，中國經濟保持平穩執行，人工智慧等新技術加快發展，面對外部衝擊出口保持較快增長，產業鏈供應鏈韌性持續彰顯，在全球主要經濟體中表現較好，各方面都普遍看好中國經濟發展前景。同時，與會專家深入分析了當前經濟執行面臨的突出困難問題，圍繞更好實施宏觀政策、積極擴大內需特別是擴大有效投資、推動科技創新和產業創新深度融合、穩定和擴大就業、防範化解重點領域風險等方面，提出了相關意見建議。

【分析師：晶片價格可能在1年內提前見頂 DRAM、NAND價格明年初或將出現連續季度下跌】近日有上市公司簽訂百億晶圓長約，披露該筆訂單“鎖量鎖價”。隨著儲存市場持續供不應求，簽訂長約（LTA）成為下游廠商保障產品供應的重要方法。摩根大通在近期釋出的一份研報裡介紹了幾類LTA合同模型。第一種是純固定價格，沒有預付款，一旦市場劇烈波動，雙方都可能毀約；第二種是固定價格+預付款，合同價通常會比第一種低一些；第三種是預付款+價格彈性機制，大部分量採用固定價格，但留出一部分量採取浮動價格機制。在長鑫、長存積極擴產，漲價造成消費電子需求下跌等背景下，雷蒙德·詹姆斯分析師卡爾·阿克曼提出新觀點：晶片價格可能在距今不到12個月的時間內提前見頂，動態隨機存取儲存器（DRAM）和快閃記憶體（NAND）價格明年初或將出現連續季度下跌。

【不足半年近200位基金經理離任 績優管理人扎堆“轉會”】今年以來，基金經理流動節奏明顯加快。Wind資料顯示，今年還不足半年（截至6月10日），已有96家基金公司近200位基金經理離任，同時，112家基金公司新聘了312位基金經理。其中，華夏基金旗下基金經理離任數量最多，年內已有11位基金經理離任，其次是嘉實基金，年內離任7位基金經理，博時基金則有6位基金經理離任。從新聘基金經理來看，易方達、富國、南方等基金公司今年以來新聘基金經理數量均超10位，易方達基金新聘基金經理高達17位，大基金公司成為人才“蓄水池”。記者注意到，“轉戰”同業成為基金經理離任後的重要去向。業內人士認為，人才流動已是當前基金業的常態，基金經理跨機構流轉，既折射出行業競爭格局的變化，也將為各家機構的投研實力帶來新變數，基金經理後續產品運作表現仍值得關注。 (新京報)

【摩根士丹利策略師預計歐洲股市有10%的上漲空間】摩根士丹利首席歐洲股票策略師Marina Zavolock表示，如果中東達成和平協議，歐洲股市有望上漲，較長期的上漲空間可達10%。她表示，今年早些時候開始流入歐洲的多元化資金在伊朗戰爭爆發後“完全停止”，投資者的資金轉而逐周流向美國。這意味著近期歐洲股市的回撥幅度也相對溫和，因為歐洲股市最初跟漲的程度較弱。Zavolock認為歐洲股市短期內最大的擔憂是夏季期間“季節性的沒有方向”，這種模式在過去十年中反覆出現。地區股市往往會經歷動能消退和空頭擠壓，從而引發市場波動。Zavolock表示，“從長遠來看，歐洲股市還有10%的上漲空間。”在當前市場波動的情況下，她加倍押注歐洲人工智慧領域，逢低買入半導體和礦業股票。

【哥斯大黎加外長：高度重視發展對華關係】哥斯大黎加外長託瓦爾9日表示，哥斯大黎加高度重視發展對華關係，願繼續推動以發展、創新、貿易和人民相知相親為導向的雙邊合作議程。中國駐哥斯大黎加大使館9日在哥首都聖何塞舉行招待會，慶祝中哥建交19週年。哥斯大黎加政府官員及社會各界代表出席活動。託瓦爾在致辭中表示，哥斯大黎加是新世紀中美洲首個同中國建交的國家，哥中自貿協定的實施讓更多優質哥斯大黎加產品進入中國市場，期待哥中進一步深化互利合作和人民友誼。中國駐哥斯大黎加大使王曉瑤表示，中哥建交19年來，兩國友誼不斷深化，各領域合作成果豐碩。中方願同哥方繼續加強政治互信，深化基礎設施、教育、衛生、科技創新和清潔能源等領域合作，更好造福兩國人民。活動期間還舉辦了哥斯大黎加國家體育場建設紀實攝影展、中國新能源汽車展以及中資企業聯合展覽，展示兩國務實合作成果。哥斯大黎加前總統菲格雷斯在接受新華社記者採訪時表示，中國在清潔能源和綠色發展領域取得顯著成就，哥中關係發展不僅有利於兩國經貿合作，也有助於雙方在環境保護等領域深化交流。哥斯大黎加前立法大會主席阿里亞斯表示，國家體育場等合作專案充分體現了兩國友好合作成果，希望雙方在基礎設施建設等領域開展更多合作，推動兩國關係不斷向前發展。（新華社）

【RBC preview of U.S. CPI: cost pass-through to consumers may keep corporate price rises through summer】RBC expects U.S. May core CPI to rise 0.3% MoM and to 2.9% YoY, citing ongoing energy-driven inflation and limited relief in food prices after recent beef-led increases. Higher aviation fuel and resilient wage growth amid a tight labor market should sustain core services inflation; core goods inflation is being supported by new and used vehicle price gains, partly masking weakness in apparel, personal care and auto parts. ISM manufacturing and services surveys show sharply rising input costs, and recent PPI suggests firms retain pricing power; RBC forecasts May headline PPI and core PPI up 0.6% MoM, to 6.3% and 5.5% YoY respectively. NFIB data show the share of firms planning price increases over the next three months near 30%, indicating cost pass-through may continue through the summer and lend further support to inflation in coming months.

【加皇銀行前瞻美國CPI：成本壓力向消費者傳導 企業漲價潮或在整個夏季持續】加拿大皇家銀行預計，能源價格上漲仍在持續推高美國整體通脹水平，食品價格領域難以出現明顯緩解，尤其是在近期牛肉價格上漲訊息頻現之後。該行預計5月核心CPI環比上漲0.3%，同比增速升至2.9%。該行認為，更高的航空燃油價格將繼續推升核心服務通脹，而勞動力市場持續緊張、工資增速保持韌性，也限制了核心服務通脹進一步回落的空間。與此同時，近幾個月核心商品通脹受到新車和二手車價格上漲支撐，而這在一定程度上掩蓋了服裝、個人護理用品以及汽車零部件等貿易敏感型商品所面臨的價格壓力。ISM製造業和服務業調查均顯示，企業投入成本正在大幅攀升。而近期PPI資料則表明，企業仍具備將更高成本轉嫁給消費者的定價能力。因此，該行預計5月份企業將繼續傳導上游成本壓力，5月整體PPI和核心PPI環比均將上漲0.6%，同比漲幅將分別升至6.3%和5.5%。此外，美國全國獨立企業聯合會（NFIB）4月調查顯示，計劃在未來三個月提高產品售價的企業比例大幅上升，接近30%。這一跡象表明，企業成本向終端價格的傳導過程可能在整個夏季持續，從而對未來數月的通脹走勢形成進一步支撐。

### 2026-06-10 18:00:24  #MKT News #Important #Macro & Rates #DXY #Impact mixed #WTI #Impact bearish

【Preview: BoC likely to hold; rate outlook remains uncertain】Market consensus is for the Bank of Canada to hold rates at the upcoming decision. Goldman expects a hold, balancing weak domestic activity against Iran-related inflationary pressure, and says the statement will likely stress the bank is "ready to act as needed" if inflation persists. JPMorgan flags a mild dovish risk to the Canadian dollar and prefers short USD/CAD positioning. Wells Fargo is an outlier, forecasting a July hike and another increase in Q4, viewing the near-term policy path as biased higher. BMO and RBC judge rates will likely remain unchanged through 2027 absent a year-end pickup in growth and employment. CIBC and Scotiabank see the economy too weak to support hikes; CIBC notes a 2026 cut could become the most likely outcome if Canada-US trade talks go poorly. BMO adds that sustained oil-price declines would increase the case for cuts next year. BBH expects the BoC to keep two-way policy options and views market pricing of >50bps of tightening over 12 months as too high. RSM warns a persistent energy-price shock could force a BoC hike this autumn. Centurion says two consecutive quarters of GDP decline are unlikely to change the bank’s hawkish rhetoric aimed at anchoring inflation expectations.

### 2026-06-10 17:48:03  #Trading Economics #Economy #Slovenia #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Slovenia Industrial Output Hits Over 1-Year High — Industrial output in Slovenia increased 4.4% year-on-year in April 2026, following a downwardly revised 2.4% rise in the previous month. Production rebounded in electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning supply (1.2% vs -15.8% in March), while manufacturing continued to grow albeit at a softer pace (4.9% vs 5.2%). Meanwhile, output in mining and quarrying slumped further (-35.2% vs -15.9%). By industrial grouping, output rebounded for intermediate goods (2.5% vs. -4.4%), while growth slowed for capital goods (8.0% vs. 11.2%) and consumer goods (5.0% vs. 8.4%). On a monthly basis, industrial activity rose 0.3% in April, easing from 4.0% in March.

Oil Prices Volatile Amid Escalating Middle East Tensions — Crude oil swung around $88 a barrel on Wednesday, after falling more than 3% in the previous session and reversing earlier gains seen during Asian trading, as traders assess escalating tensions in the Middle East. Iran launched attacks on several Gulf nations, including Bahrain, Jordan, and Kuwait, following US “self-defence strikes” in response to the downing of an American helicopter. Traffic through the Strait of Hormuz remains significantly disrupted, with Iran blocking most shipping through the key waterway, while the US has imposed its own restrictions on Iranian ports. However, US Energy Secretary Chris Wright said vessel traffic in the Gulf and oil exports through the Strait are rising despite ongoing disruptions. Meanwhile, API data showed US crude inventories fell by 9.1 million barrels last week to their lowest level in four months, as buyers moved to replace supplies disrupted by tensions in the Persian Gulf.

### 2026-06-10 17:36:03  #其他

【亨通光電：CPO保偏光纖實現重大核心技術突破】據亨通光電訊息，近日，亨通光電自主研發的CPO保偏光纖實現重大核心技術突破。依託HPPD高精度保偏光纖拉絲技術，亨通透過精密張力調控與線上實時補償系統，將裸纖直徑控制精度提升至±0.1μm，大幅優於傳統工藝精度。超高的光纖直徑一致性，有效解決了高密度光纖陣列的誤差放大問題，保障FAU高密度封裝場景下的光纖定位精度與矽光晶片耦合效率，適配高階CPO裝置的嚴苛裝配需求。透過HPPQ高精度石英加工技術，亨通精準調控光纖“貓眼”應力區的尺寸與位置，徹底解決行業普遍存在的“貓眼大小眼”頑疾，將應力區對稱度控制在0.2μm以內，精準達成“三芯一線”精密對準標準。該技術大幅提升光纖偏振穩定性與消光比效能，從源頭端保障外接鐳射光源接入CPO系統的極低串擾，保障高速光訊號高質量、穩定傳輸。

### 2026-06-10 17:25:57  #Trading Economics #Economy #Greece #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Greece Inflation Eases Slightly in May — The annual inflation rate in Greece edged down to 5.2% in May 2026 from 5.4% in the prior month. Price growth slowed in housing and utilities (11.6% vs 13.8% in April), food and non-alcoholic beverages (3.5% vs 4.4%). Decreases were also observed in alcoholic beverages and tobacco (-0.1% vs 1.4%), clothing and footwear (-0.9% vs 2.0%), furnishings and household equipment (-0.1% vs 0.1%), and information and communication (-2.7% vs -2.2%). Meanwhile, inflation accelerated for transport (11.5% vs 10.0%), restaurants and accommodation (8.5% vs 6.1%), recreation and culture (1.4% vs 1.0%), while remained steady for education (at 2.8%) and health (at 1.2%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices were unchanged after rising 1.5% in the previous month. Meanwhile, the EU-harmonised annual inflation rate rose to 4.9%, the highest since March 2023, and up from 4.6% previously.

### 2026-06-10 17:20:32  #Trading Economics #Economy #Greece #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Greece Industrial Output Growth Eases in April — Industrial output in Greece rose 2.1% year-on-year in April 2026, easing from a downwardly revised twenty-month high of 8.2% in the previous month. Manufacturing growth slowed to 1.0% from 5.5%, reflecting weaker production in food products (0.8% vs 8.9%), textiles (10.4% vs 11.0%), wearing apparel (4.9% vs 14.8%), and chemicals (0.2% vs 3.6%). Additionally, production decreased in electricity supply (9.8% vs 24.6%). Output returned to growth in water supply (0.1% vs -1.5%), while the decline in mining and quarrying eased (-2.8% vs -5.4%). By industrial grouping, growth moderated in energy (9.0% vs 12.1%) and turned negative for consumer non-durables (-1.5% vs 12.0%) and durables (-12.0% vs 19.5%). Meanwhile, output growth accelerated in intermediate goods (1.9% vs 0.6%) and capital goods (6.0% vs 4.4%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, industrial activity fell 3.5% in April, following an upwardly revised 1.4% gain in the preceding period.

【美國5月CPI或創三年多來新高 消費者信心和購買力持續遭侵蝕】經濟學家預計，週三公佈的一項關鍵通脹指標將顯示，美國5月份消費者價格漲幅創三年多來新高。 穆迪分析公司首席經濟學家馬克·贊迪稱，與新冠疫情危機期間由供應中斷引發的通脹不同，最近這波物價上漲主要歸因於包括伊朗戰爭在內的政府政策。 他說，“距離通脹上次達到美聯儲的目標已經過去了將近五年，我認為這已經逐漸侵蝕了集體心理，這也是人們對經濟感到如此糟糕的原因之一。”根據哥倫比亞廣播公司新聞最近的一項民意調查，四分之三的美國人表示他們的收入跟不上通貨膨脹 。經濟學家表示，5月份的CPI資料顯示，能源價格上漲可能是導致持續通脹的主要原因。贊迪表示，除了能源成本外，經濟學家們週三還將重點關注商品和服務成本，以評估更高的燃料價格如何影響經濟。 他說：“不僅僅是汽油價格上漲，柴油價格也上漲，這推高了所有用卡車運輸的貨物的價格，從食品雜貨到亞馬遜包裹都包括在內。現在坐飛機也更貴了，因為航空公司已經把更高的航空燃油成本轉嫁給了乘客。”

【US May CPI likely highest in over three years; energy seen as key driver】Economists expect Wednesday's US May CPI to show consumer prices rising at their fastest pace in more than three years. Moody's Analytics chief economist Mark Zandi said, unlike pandemic-era inflation driven by supply disruptions, the recent surge is largely linked to government policy including the Iran war and is eroding consumer confidence. A CBS poll found 75% of Americans say their incomes are not keeping up with inflation. Economists say energy price gains are the main contributor to persistent inflation and will watch goods and services components to assess pass-through; higher diesel is lifting trucking costs and prices for transported goods from groceries to e‑commerce parcels, and higher jet fuel has been passed through into airfares.

【國際航協：中東航線中斷和油價高企致航空業盈利減半】6月10日，根據國際航空運輸協會最新全球航空業財務展望，由於戰爭導致中東航線中斷和油價高企，航空業盈利能力減半。國際航協理事長威利·沃爾什（Willie Walsh）表示：由中東戰爭導致的航線運營中斷和燃油成本上升，使航空公司的前景轉差。全球來看，航空公司盈利預計將較2025年減半，利潤將從2025年的450億美元降至今年的230億美元，利潤率也將從4.2%降至2.0%。航空燃油價格快速激增70%，正在衝擊所有航空公司的盈利。部分額外成本正透過價格調整和效率提升得到回收，但這不足以將盈利能力維持在上一年水平。那些年初資產負債表已經較弱的小型航空公司顯然正面臨困難。從區域看，除中東地區外，所有地區將實現盈利，但財務表現將大幅削弱。

【伊朗媒體：美軍空襲導致伊朗南部至少兩萬居民斷水】據伊朗塔斯尼姆社10日報道，伊朗南部霍爾木茲甘省兩處關鍵供水基礎設施在日前美軍發動的空襲中遭導彈擊中破壞，導致當地至少2萬名居民的飲用水供應完全中斷。霍爾木茲甘省水務公司執行長證實，遭襲擊破壞的民用設施為巴馬尼地區的兩座混凝土蓄水池，總容量為2500立方米。這兩座蓄水池承擔著庫赫斯塔克鎮及周邊10個村莊的全部生活用水供應，目前受損設施已完全停止執行。報道稱，襲擊造成的直接基礎設施經濟損失估計約為1400億託曼（約合79.5萬美元）。由於事發地區近期氣溫高達45攝氏度至50攝氏度，且地下水資源匱乏，當地民眾的飲水安全正面臨嚴峻挑戰。目前，伊朗有關部門已啟動應急機制，透過調集油罐車運送生活用水並部署臨時替代抽水系統，全力緩解受災地區的斷水危機。

【埃及蘇伊士運河油輪通行量增長 成為霍爾木茲海峽關閉的意外受益者】霍爾木茲海峽關閉促使能源運輸改道至紅海替代航線，受此影響，今年4月透過埃及蘇伊士運河的油輪數量激增近三分之一，帶動運河收入升至2024年初以來最高水平。埃及國家統計局資料顯示，當月共有529艘油輪透過運河，同比增加28%。整體航運量也有所上升，所有型別船隻共計1,182艘，同比增長14%。儘管兩年前葉門胡塞武裝開始襲擊紅海南部航運後，蘇伊士運河通行量曾明顯下滑，但埃及最新資料表明，美以對伊朗戰爭的外溢效應，意外利好蘇伊士運河。投資銀行EFG Hermes宏觀經濟分析主管Mohamed Abu Basha表示，蘇伊士運河“正成為這一地區衝突中出人意料的淨受益者”。CAPMAS資料顯示，4月蘇伊士運河收入達4.19億美元，同比增長27%，創下自2024年初胡塞武裝升級襲擊航運以來的最高月度水平。運河歷來是埃及的重要外匯來源，與旅遊業和海外匯款並列。

【滙豐上調全球股票至最高超配】滙豐環球投資研究首席多資產策略師馬克斯・凱特納於6月8日釋出研報，重申該行看多全球股票的立場。他認為，當前投資者重點擔憂的各類風險，要麼已基本被市場消化，要麼被過度放大。 據悉，這份研報進一步強化了滙豐近數月以來的一貫配置策略：全球股票配置上調至最高超配級別，重點看好亞洲新興市場、日本及歐洲股市（尤其歐洲銀行股）；同時對新興市場本幣債券大幅超配，並看多高收益信用債。凱特納研報的核心觀點是：當前市場熱議的風險並非新訊息。貿易衝突、地區衝突、通脹壓力、經濟增速放緩、人工智慧板塊估值偏高，這些問題數月來早已被反覆討論。該行表示：“在我們看來，利空訊息逐步減少，就足以支撐行情向好。”除市場情緒邏輯外，滙豐也指出了實打實的基本面利好。美國高頻經濟資料與就業市場表現穩健，退稅總額較2025年同期高出近15%，為居民消費增添緩衝空間。 盈利層面，滙豐認為全球企業盈利前景比地緣政治局勢更具決定性。

### 2026-06-10 16:49:25  #宏觀 #焦點

【新國標釋出：填補雲端計算在我國GLP實驗室合規應用領域標準空白】市場監管總局近日批准釋出《良好實驗室規範實施要求 第8部分：雲端計算在良好實驗室規範試驗機構中的應用》國家標準。該標準是我國良好實驗室規範（GLP）系列標準的重要組成部分，填補了雲端計算技術在我國GLP實驗室合規應用領域的標準空白，標誌著我國GLP體系建設邁入數字化轉型與資訊科技融合的新階段。該標準系統規定了GLP試驗機構在引入雲端計算服務時的應用要求與管理指南。一是明確試驗機構對GLP符合性承擔不可轉移的最終責任，並細化機構管理者、系統管理員、檔案管理員及質量保證人員在雲環境下的具體管理要求。二是詳細界定“基礎設施即服務”“平臺即服務”“軟體即服務”三種服務模式下，試驗機構與雲服務提供商之間計算資源的管理責任歸屬，確保責任鏈條清晰可查。三是構建涵蓋風險評估、供應商評估、服務級別協議和系統驗證的實施框架，為雲服務選型、協議簽訂到系統退役提供全流程合規指引。四是規範退出策略與電子存檔要求，確保資料在雲服務終止或系統退役時能夠完整歸檔或安全遷移。該標準的釋出實施，是對我國現有GLP國家標準體系的補充和完善，使我國GLP技術要求和國際經濟合作與發展組織（OECD）最新要求保持協調一致，為保障GLP試驗機構安全、合規引入雲端計算技術提供標準化支撐。 (央視新聞)

### 2026-06-10 16:41:23  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Rubber Futures at Near 2-Week low — Rubber futures eased to around 225 US cents per kilogram, the lowest since late May, pressured by rising supply prospects and weak seasonal demand. Asian natural rubber producers like Thailand, Indonesia and Malaysia are currently in the main tapping season, which typically runs from around May to October. During this period, higher rainfall and more favourable growing conditions enable rubber trees to produce more latex, increasing supply. Supply in the synthetic rubber segment is expected to rise as Chinese butadiene rubber producers restart operations at higher capacity following maintenance. On the demand side, analysts note that the automotive market typically weakens in summer as consumers shift spending toward travel and leisure, reducing tyre demand and weighing on rubber prices.

### 2026-06-10 16:37:35  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #US Economy #Commodities #Energy #DXY #Impact bullish #US10Y #Impact bearish #S&P500

【May US CPI seen back above 4% YoY; Charles Schwab says inflation not just oil-driven】Wednesday’s US CPI is expected to show May headline +0.5% MoM, lifting YoY inflation to about 4.2% — the first reading above 4% since May 2023 and the highest since April 2023, according to a Dow Jones survey. Core CPI ex-food and energy is expected +0.3% MoM and +2.9% YoY. Energy-driven price jumps tied to the Iran war are a major factor, but markets are increasingly concerned that inflation is broadening beyond oil as pass-through and rising inflation expectations spread. Charles Schwab Chief Investment Strategist Liz Ann Sonders said the issue is not just oil but also monetary supply and, increasingly, AI-related factors, warning that inflation may remain sticky. Sonders added investor anxiety is concentrated on inflation and that equities would likely struggle if data disappoints. While the Trump administration has argued inflation would fall if Middle East tensions ease, Sonders cautioned supply-side disruptions could keep oil and inflation elevated even after hostilities subside.

### 2026-06-10 16:32:24  #宏觀

【城市軌道交通車輛“體檢標準” 新規釋出 今年11月起實施】記者今天瞭解到，市場監管總局近日批准釋出新修訂的國家標準《城市軌道交通車輛組裝後的檢查與試驗規則》，自2026年11月1日起正式實施。城市軌道交通車輛是城市公共交通的運力核心，其安全性直接關係到億萬乘客的生命財產安全。該標準覆蓋地鐵車輛、輕軌車輛、市域快速軌道交通車輛等多種主流車型，是城市軌道交通車輛製造完成、正式載客運營前必須透過的“體檢標準”。本次修訂主要包括以下四個方面的內容：築牢安全底線，細化全維度安全驗證要求。該標準對消防安全、電氣安全、電磁安全、應急逃生、車輛救援等方面提出更嚴格的試驗要求，全面夯實車輛執行安全基礎。聚焦民生體驗，全面升級乘車舒適標準。該標準新增甲醛、總揮發性有機物（TVOC）車內空氣質量檢測，最佳化空調、照明、噪聲控制標準，完善車輛平穩性、加減速效能及車門效能測試，提高乘客出行舒適度。踐行綠色理念，助力行業低碳轉型發展。該標準細化車輛阻力、能耗試驗要求，推動車輛輕量化和節能牽引技術應用，有效降低運營能耗，助力城市軌道交通領域碳減排。緊跟智慧化趨勢，完善全系統試驗要求。該標準細化車門、乘客資訊等系統聯動試驗要求，新增全自動執行功能測試，為城市軌道交通車輛裝置自動化、智慧化升級提供標準依據。該標準的釋出實施將指導構建更安全、更智慧、更綠色、更舒適的城市軌道交通車輛檢驗技術體系，推動城市軌道交通車輛裝置更新、技術升級與產業提質增效，助力我國城市軌道交通裝備國產化、標準化、高階化發展。（央視新聞）

### 2026-06-10 16:30:31  #公司

【星艦成SpaceX估值支柱 火星願景仍待驗證】埃隆・馬斯克懷揣載人登陸火星的宏偉願景，同時力求讓SpaceX完成史上規模最大的首次公開募股，而這兩大目標的核心載體，正是星艦——一枚高達407英尺的巨型火箭。 這家由全球首富執掌、業務橫跨人工智慧與衛星領域的綜合企業集團，已投入超150億美元研發這款人類有史以來體量最大的火箭。在本月提交的上市監管檔案中，SpaceX將星艦視作公司未來發展規劃的核心支柱。 SpaceX希望星艦能成為全球首套完全可重複使用的發射系統。該火箭兩級箭體均可回收複用，且有效載荷能力大幅提升。這將幫助公司以遠更低的成本向軌道運送更多裝置、發射更大體積的衛星，最終執行登月與火星探測任務。SpaceX表示，若想繼續擴張星鏈網路，就必須依靠星艦。星艦能夠搭載現役火箭無法運送的大體積、高效能衛星。 但在此之前，公司還需接連攻克諸多工程技術難關。 截至目前，星艦已開展12次試飛，主要用於測試核心零部件與整體系統。多次試飛均以失敗告終：曾出現箭體失控、殘骸墜落至加勒比海部分割槽域的事故，還有一枚火箭在地面點火測試時發生爆炸。 挑戰並不僅僅在於造出星艦，更在於實現空前高頻次的常態化發射。SpaceX發展至今，已累計向太空運送數千噸載荷。而想要落地那些宏偉規劃，就需要運送體量更為龐大的物資，這意味著星艦每年可能要執行數千次發射任務。

### 2026-06-10 16:24:37  #MKT News #Market Themes #AI Revolution #Chip Wars #Market Regions #Greater China #Nasdaq100 #Impact bullish #CSI300 #Impact mixed

【Guojin Securities' Mu Yiling: Market enters high-volatility; favor active defense, dividends and upstream tech】Guojin Securities chief strategist Mu Yiling says AI capex as a share of global GDP is rising rapidly and has real cyclical support, comparable to China’s 2005–07 urbanization-driven growth cycle; capital is concentrating in AI hardware. He sees room for further gains in the Philadelphia Semiconductor Index, while A-share growth valuations have expanded faster than US peers and some profits are priced near the most optimistic levels of the 2005–07 cycle—yet valuation alone does not imply a market top and the market lacks rotation conditions. The AI narrative is weighing on growth outside the US; A-shares are hitting new highs even as breadth (rising-stock share) contracts. Mu judges the market has entered a high-volatility regime similar to early 2007 and recommends an active-defensive allocation: priority to dividend sectors (oil, coal, power, city commercial banks), secondary emphasis on upstream tech (semiconductors/AI materials, equipment and manufacturing); medium-term, once AI headwinds ease, favor industrial metals, general and specialized machinery and export-oriented names.

### 2026-06-10 16:22:44  #其他

【全球監管機構呼籲加強對金融領域智慧體化AI的管控】隨著人工智慧(AI)加快普及，全球監管機構表示，自主性日益增強的AI形態可能加劇金融體系風險，並呼籲制定新的管控措施。全球標準制定機構金融穩定理事會 (FSB) 週三釋出的一份報告“強烈”鼓勵各機構考慮實施防護措施，以減輕AI帶來的風險，包括“智慧體化AI”(Agentic AI)帶來的風險。智慧體化AI是指那些能夠在有限的人工監督下進行規劃、推理並執行任務的系統。金融機構已將智慧體化AI用於欺詐檢測、客戶服務和後臺運營等領域。劍橋另類金融中心(Cambridge Centre for Alternative Finance)的一項調查顯示，在金融行業受訪者中，有52%表示正在積極採用智慧體化AI，其中23%已在規模化推廣或推進轉型，29%則處於試點階段。自Anthropic釋出Mythos以來，監管機構和全球標準制定機構加大了對AI在金融領域推廣所帶來的風險的警示力度。專家認為，Mythos給銀行業帶來重大網路安全挑戰。FSB表示，自主性AI會帶來可能“以極快速度顯現”的風險，包括未經授權或非法行為、資料洩露以及對互聯絡統的干擾。

Bulgaria Construction Output Up 6.6% YoY — Construction output in Bulgaria rose 6.6% year-on-year in April 2026, following a downwardly revised 6.5% rise in March. Bulgaria’s Industrial Output Falls in April — Bulgaria’s industrial production declined 4.1% year over year in April 2026, partially offsetting March’s 4.7% growth, driven by reductions in capital goods (-3.2% vs. -5.1% in March), intermediate goods (-5.9% vs. -2.6%), and consumer durables (-3.2% vs. -5.1%). Growth also slowed for energy goods (3.3% vs. 52.9%) and consumer non-durables (1.1% vs. 4.3%). Month over month, output fell 4.6%, following a 5.2% rise in March.

### 2026-06-10 16:20:50  #Trading Economics #Economy #Italy #Industrial Production Mom #Importance 1

Italy Industrial Output Grows 0.5% in April — Italy’s industrial production increased by 0.5% month-on-month in April 2026, defying market expectations of a 0.1% fall, following a downwardly revised 0.6% increase in March. Growth in capital goods production slowed (1.0% vs 2.1% in March), while output of intermediate goods rose further (0.8% vs 0.3%). Smaller declines were also observed in the production of consumer goods (-0.1% vs -0.4%) and energy (-0.2% vs -1.2%). On a yearly basis, industrial output grew by 1.3% after a downwardly revised 1.4% gain in March. Production expanded strongly for capital goods (6.4% vs 5.6%) and intermediate goods (1.8% vs 0.4%), while energy (-2.7% vs -3.1%) and consumer goods (-4.1% vs -1.9%) registered declines. By industry, the strongest growth was recorded in the manufacture of transport equipment (17.8%), basic pharmaceutical products and pharmaceutical preparations (7.9%), and machinery and equipment (6.1%).

### 2026-06-10 16:15:14  #市場 #國際

【德國商業銀行：SpaceX首次公開募股將吸引大量資本流入 進一步支撐美元走勢】德國商業銀行日前釋出的一份分析報告顯示，美國SpaceX即將進行的首次公開募股（IPO）預計將吸引鉅額國際資本流入美國，在單一交易日內可為美國季度經常賬戶赤字的8%提供再融資，從而對美元、尤其是美元兌歐元匯率形成有力支撐。根據德國商業銀行貨幣策略師主管沃爾克馬爾·鮑爾（Volkmar Baur）團隊的測算，SpaceX此次IPO計劃以1.75萬億美元的估值集資750億美元。由於國際投資者平均持有美股約20%的份額，且歐洲投資者佔其中一半，考慮到歐洲市場缺乏同類科技替代股，海外資金對科技巨頭IPO的參與度通常更高。分析預計，本週五（6月12日）IPO啟動當天，至少將有150億美元的海外資本流入美國市場。報告將這一資金規模與美國宏觀經濟資料進行了對比。資料顯示，美國上季度經常賬戶赤字達1907億美元，這意味著SpaceX單日吸引的海外資金流入量，即可滿足美國季度經常賬戶赤字8%的再融資需求。鮑爾明確指出，若上述跨境資本有半數來自歐洲，將直接對美元形成利好，拉動美元兌歐元震盪走強。德國商業銀行策略團隊強調，當前以人工智慧和高科技為代表的IPO熱潮不僅在微觀層面支撐了美國經濟，也在宏觀層面為美元流動性提供了重要支撐。隨著未來鎖定期結束，更多SpaceX股票將投放市場，加之 Anthropic 和 OpenAI 等人工智慧企業已向市場傳導了明確的上市預期，國際資本流入美國實體及金融市場的趨勢在未來數月內仍將延續。

【Moore Threads open-sources MusaCoder, first code LLM fully trained on domestic full‑stack GPUs】Moore Threads has released and open‑sourced MusaCoder, a code‑focused large language model optimized for GPU‑level operator generation. 【合肥科學島團隊在高效穩定倒置型鈣鈦礦太陽能電池研究方面取得新進展】近期，中國科學院合肥物質院固體所陳沖研究員團隊在鈣鈦礦太陽能電池效率與穩定性協同提升研究方面取得重要進展。研究團隊創新性地提出了一種基於還原型谷胱甘肽（GSH）多功能新增劑的“動態調控+靜態防護”協同穩定策略，有效緩解了倒置型鈣鈦礦太陽能電池長期存在的效率與穩定性難以兼顧的核心難題。相關研究成果發表在Advanced Materials上。

### 2026-06-10 15:57:57  #MKT News

【State Administration for Market Regulation to accelerate mutual recognition with international standards】The State Administration for Market Regulation (SAMR) said China will deepen alignment with international rules and accelerate mutual recognition of standards and conformity-assessment systems, prioritising key partner regions and major trading partners. SAMR will proactively benchmark international conformity-assessment rules, technical regulations and standards, and speed up alignment of domestic certification rules, testing methods and evaluation metrics with global practice while participating in international rule‑setting to facilitate cross‑border trade. The agency plans to establish mutual‑recognition mechanisms to clear the ‘last mile’ of implementation, promote international recognition of certification results, cross‑border acceptance of testing data and shared use of audit experience, aiming for a “one test, one certification, multi‑country acceptance, global circulation” model and to have domestic certification results included in international acceptance lists. SAMR said these measures are intended to lower certification costs, shorten certification cycles and expand overseas acceptance to address exporters’ difficulties, high certification expenses and lack of interoperability.

### 2026-06-10 15:49:36  #其他

【機構稱800VDC與CPO落地時間都將延後 預計Scale-up CPO大規模匯入時間將在2029年】AI產業鏈明星分析機構SemiAnalysis最新發布的一篇報告稱800VDC與CPO落地時間都將延後。800VDC方面，該報告指出，這一架構遭到超大規模雲廠商質疑，部署進度正在放緩，相關產品量產出貨時間已延遲至2028年以後，同時400VDC方案仍在按計劃推進下半年落地。CPO方面，該報告直言2027年落地的市場預期過於樂觀，實際部署進度將晚於華爾街當前普遍預測。報告計劃下調2026年和2027年的Scale-out CPO出貨預測，預計Scale-up CPO大規模匯入時間將在2029年，而市場此前普遍預期在2027-2028年。相較之下，未來幾年更多將是NPO專案進入量產。

【US CPI preview: inflation drivers extend beyond oil; stickiness likely to persist】Markets expect US May headline CPI 4.2% YoY (Apr 2.4%), 0.5% MoM — the first print above 4% since May 2023 and the highest since Apr 2023. The headline rise is driven primarily by energy after the Iran-related Middle East conflict; core CPI ex-food and energy is seen at 2.9% YoY, 0.3% MoM. Charles Schwab Wealth Management chief strategist Sanders says inflation is not just an oil problem but also reflects monetary supply and factors increasingly linked to AI, raising the risk of persistent inflation; she warns a stronger-than-expected print would pressure equities. The Trump administration expects inflation to fall once Middle East fighting eases, but Sanders cautions supply damage may prevent oil from reverting quickly to prior lows even if the conflict ends.

### 2026-06-10 15:41:01  #Trading Economics #Economy #Moldova #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Moldova Inflation Rate Steady at 6.8% in May — The annual inflation rate in Moldova stood at 6.8% in May 2026, unchanged from the previous month, and remained the highest level since December 2025. Prices of non-food products accelerated (7.2% vs 7.1% in April), driven by strong increases in fuels and motor fuels, footwear, carpets and other floor coverings, and medicines. At the same time, price growth in services edged up (7.5% vs 6.9%), supported by increases in passenger transport specifically in urban and air, regulated tariffs for electricity supply, rent, education and public catering services. Meanwhile, inflation for food products eased to 5.7% from 6.3% in the previous month, due to notable decreases in vegetables, fresh fruits, eggs, although costs for fish and canned fish, dairy products, and alcoholic beverages continued to rise. On a monthly basis, consumer prices increased 0.4% in May, slowing from a 1.8% rise in April.

【我國“一次評價、多國通行”的互認機制已覆蓋全球經濟總量超95%區域】記者今天（10日）從市場監管總局召開的新聞釋出會上瞭解到，我國透過加強質量認證領域的國際合作，不斷深化國際互認，在促進國際貿易降低成本、提升效率等方面取得了積極成效。在多邊合作方面，市場監管總局已簽署15份多邊互認協議，並推薦84家認證和檢驗檢測機構加入國際電工委員會（IEC）合格評定體系，每年發放電子元器件、電工、防爆電器、可再生能源類產品認證證書、報告一萬餘張，“一次評價、多國通行”的互認機制可覆蓋全球經濟總量超95%的區域，有力服務我國外貿企業走向國際市場。在雙邊合作方面，市場監管總局已簽署159份合格評定領域雙邊合作檔案，與超過40個國家和地區建立起雙邊合作安排。今年以來，先後與英國、西班牙、巴基斯坦有關部門簽署了備忘錄，在資訊交流、能力建設等方面深化合作。在國家間協議框架下，目前我國已有29家認證機構與98家境外機構建立起常態化合作機制，實施的跨境認證專案覆蓋38個國家和地區，核發國際合作認證證書近12萬張。（央視新聞）

### 2026-06-10 15:24:36  #Trading Economics #Economy #India #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

India 10Y Yield Falls to Near Two-Month Low — The yield on India’s 10-year G-Sec fell to around 6.8%, its lowest level in nearly two months, as renewed foreign investor demand lifted sentiment in the debt market. Foreign investors purchased nearly INR 10,000 of Indian bonds over the last four trading sessions, supported by the government's decision to fully exempt taxes on gains from eligible debt investments and the Reserve Bank of India's move to expand the range of securities available to overseas investors. The inflows mark a turnaround after FPIs had net sold more than INR 10,119 of Indian debt since the start of the US-Israel conflict involving Iran. Bond yields also came under pressure from expectations of RBI support for the rupee. Traders indicated the central bank likely intervened after recent currency weakness, reportedly selling dollars in the spot market while conducting dollar-rupee buy/sell swaps with maturities exceeding one year through state-run banks.

### 2026-06-10 15:21:03  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Inflation Rate #Importance 3

US Inflation Rate Set to Reach Highest Level Since 2023 — The annual inflation rate in the US is expected to rise to 4.2% in May 2026, marking its highest level since April 2023, from 3.8% in April. This would represent the third consecutive monthly acceleration in headline inflation, driven largely by higher gasoline prices following the energy shock triggered by the conflict with Iran. However, the broader pass-through to consumer prices is expected to remain relatively limited. On a monthly basis, consumer prices are projected to increase by 0.5% in May, following a 0.6% rise in April. Gasoline prices likely went up around 9%, but the fading impact of a one-off adjustment to rent data should have a moderating effect on inflation. Meanwhile, core inflation, which excludes volatile food and energy prices, is expected to edge up to 2.9% year-on-year, a fresh-high since September 2025, from 2.8% in April. On a monthly basis, core consumer prices are estimated to have risen by 0.3%, after increasing 0.4% in the previous month.

### 2026-06-10 15:21:01  #Trading Economics #Economy #China #Total Vehicle Sales #Importance 1

China Car Sales Drop 2.1% in May — China’s total vehicle sales fell 2.1% year-on-year in May 2026, following a 2.5% decline in the previous month, according to data from the China Association of Automobile Manufacturers (CAAM). New energy vehicle (NEV) sales rose 14.4% year-on-year to 1.496 million units in May, accelerating from 9.7% growth in April, with NEVs accounting for 56.9% of total vehicle sales. Meanwhile, domestic sales plunged 20.4% year-on-year in May. In contrast, vehicle exports surged 68.7%, serving as a key driver of the sector, which has increasingly relied on overseas shipments to offset weakness in the domestic market. Last year, China’s vehicle sales increased 9.4% year-on-year to a record high of 34.4 million units, up from a 4.5% increase in 2024, while NEV sales surged 28.2%.

Wheat Futures Rise on El Niño Weather Risks — Wheat futures rose above $5.90 per bushel in early June, staying away from a near two-month low touched on June 5, as the emergence of El Niño heightened concerns over global crop risks. The climate phenomenon, which has taken shape across the equatorial Pacific and expected to intensify in the months ahead, is likely to unleash droughts, floods, and temperature extremes that could disrupt production of key commodities, including wheat. Its impacts are also projected to intensify globally, including a wetter-than-usual winter in the southern US, drought and wildfire risks in Australia, and potential disruptions to the Atlantic hurricane season. Additional support came from short-covering and bargain hunting ahead of the USDA’s closely watched June supply-and-demand report due on Thursday, as investors monitored signs of renewed Chinese purchases of US agricultural products following Beijing’s pledge last month to buy $17 billion worth of US farm goods.

### 2026-06-10 15:20:29  #Trading Economics #Economy #Austria #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Austria Industrial Output Growth Eases in April — Industrial production in Austria rose 0.6% year-on-year in April 2026, slowing from a 1.7% reading in the previous month. The increase was driven mainly by a rebound in the construction sector (1.6% vs -0.7% in March) and a modest increase in industrial production (0.4% vs 0.3%). Among the main industrial groupings, capital goods recorded the strongest growth (4.0% vs 2.5%). Output growth slowed for intermediate goods (1.9% vs 2.0%) and consumer durables (4.0% vs 9.2%). Meanwhile, the decline in energy output eased (-5.2% vs -11.3%), while consumer non-durables fell at a faster pace (-5.8% vs -3.0%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, industrial output decreased 0.1% in April.

### 2026-06-10 15:18:32  #Trading Economics #Economy #Slovakia #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Slovakia Industrial Output Drops Faster in April — Industrial production in Slovakia dropped by 3.2% year-on-year in April 2026, better than market expectations for a 3.5% contraction, though a sharper decline than the slightly downwardly revised 0.8% fall in March. The latest reading marked the third consecutive month of contraction in industrial activity and the steepest decline in the current downturn, as output fell in 10 of the 15 monitored industrial sectors. The manufacture of transport equipment remained the biggest drag on production, with output declining by 5.4% (vs -5.3% in March), followed by an 8.1% drop (vs -4.1%) in the manufacture of basic metals and an 11.4% fall (vs -45.7%) in coke and refined petroleum products. Partly offsetting these declines were gains in the manufacture of electrical equipment (5% vs 9.8%), and machinery and equipment (3.4% vs 3.2%). On a seasonally adjusted basis, industrial production was unchanged from the previous month.

### 2026-06-10 15:17:36  #Trading Economics #Economy #Czech Republic #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Czech Republic Inflation Rate at Confirmed at 2.1% — The annual inflation rate in the Czech Republic eased to 2.1% year-on-year in May 2026 from 2.5% in the previous month, confirming preliminary estimates. Price growth moderated across several categories, including alcoholic beverages and tobacco (3.7% vs 5.2% in April), recreation, sports and culture (3.0% vs 3.4%), health (2.8% vs 4.0%), information and communication (2.5% vs 3.4%), furnishings, household equipment and routine maintenance (1.1% vs 1.4%), and personal care, social protection, and miscellaneous goods and services (3.3% vs 3.5%). Meanwhile, deflation persisted for food and non-alcoholic beverages (-1.9% vs -1.3%) and clothing and footwear (-2.5% vs -2.4%). Conversely, inflation increased for housing and utilities (1.3% vs 1.2%), transport (9.4% vs 8.7%), education (4.0% vs 3.9%), and restaurants and hotels (4.4% vs 4.3%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices eased 0.1% in May from a 0.5% increase in April and marking the softest monthly gain since February.

【千問釋出高考志願填報Agent，免費提供志願填報諮詢】千問上線首個全週期高考志願填報Agent，為全國考生免費提供志願填報和諮詢服務。基於千問高考志願大模型和夸克8年高考資料經驗打造，高考Agent具備“志願報告”、“志願日曆”、“志願問答”三項核心能力，相當於為每位考生配備了一位貼身的AI高考志願填報專家。 據悉，考生向千問提供選科、估分等基礎資訊後，即可在“志願日曆”的規劃下，一步步形成對專業院校、性格偏好和志願方案的深入瞭解，並免費獲取定製化的“高考志願報告”，涵蓋填報所需的幾十種志願組合，可直接寫入填報系統。類似的深度志願服務，在市場上收費往往超過5000元。 為確保內容的專業性與準確性，高考志願填報Agent基於千問高考志願大模型打造，並結合了夸克8年高考服務積累的資料與經驗。 千問事業部產品負責人鄭嗣壽介紹，中國每年有超過1000萬考生參加高考。其中為考生聘請專業志願填報師的家庭佔比不到5%，剩下95%的家庭和考生更多靠自己的摸索完成志願填報。“千問希望能為每位考生提供一位免費的AI高考志願填報專家”。（新浪科技）

### 2026-06-10 15:12:35  #國際

【中國國際問題研究院釋出《推動全球文明對話的中國行動》專題研究報告】2026年6月10日，在第二個“文明對話國際日”到來之際，中國國際問題研究院正式釋出《推動全球文明對話的中國行動》專題研究報告。2023年3月，習近平主席提出全球文明倡議，呼籲各方共同倡導尊重世界文明多樣性、共同倡導弘揚全人類共同價值、共同倡導重視文明傳承和創新、共同倡導加強國際人文交流合作，為促進人類文明進步提供了中國方案。2024年6 月，第78屆聯合國大會協商一致透過中國等83個國家提出的決議，將每年6月10日設立為“文明對話國際日”，得到 國際社會熱烈響應和廣泛支援。為進一步梳理總結中國推動落實全球文明倡議、促進全 球文明對話的經驗做法和主要成就，中國國際問題研究院撰 寫了《推動全球文明對話的中國行動》專題研究報告。報告介紹了全球文明倡議提出的時代背景和深刻意涵，從廣泛凝 聚文明交流互鑑的國際共識、支援聯合國發揮文明對話主平 臺作用、深入開展雙邊和區域文明對話、開展多邊文明對話 合作、將文明對話融入各領域交流合作五個方面系統梳理中國推動文明對話的理念政策和實踐成果，並展望了中方推動文明對話的下步行動。（外交部）

【廣發證券：出口高增長的宏觀效應有四】廣發證券研報稱，5月出口同比增長19.4%，進一步超過前4個月累計增速的14.5%。去年同期基數偏低有一定貢獻；但從環比來看，5月也略高於過去十年均值。出口強仍是一個毫無疑問的結論。出口高增長的宏觀效應有四：一是總量託底效應，中國經濟的出口依存度2025年為19%左右，出口偏強會對部分行業的景氣度和盈利帶來一定帶動，在固投和消費偏弱的背景下，對經濟形成一個基礎性的託底；二是價格傳遞效應，本月手機出口價格的上行是一個訊號。從歷史規律看，出口價格指數略滯後於PPI週期，目前價格處於上行週期，這對於年內含價的出口額增速來說是一個安全墊，對於有品牌護城河和產品競爭力的行業盈利來說也是一個有利條件；三是政策空間效應，在出口高增長背景下，宏觀經濟政策不易有系統性的刺激，會更側重於對固投、一般消費的補短板；四是產業驗證效應，AI帶動的半導體領域的高出口增速繼續驗證“美國高資本開支增速-亞洲高訂單和產品增速”的迴圈，對於AI產業鏈定價的分子端邏輯是一個支援。

### 2026-06-10 14:47:17  #公司

【鋰礦大訊息引發鋰市場異動】一則有關寧德時代梘下窩鋰礦用地手續的資訊，引發鋰礦市場異動。6月8日，江西省自然資源廳釋出公告，依法登出編號為“用字第3609002025XS0001571號”的《建設專案用地預審與選址意見書》。公告顯示，該登出系根據宜春時代新能源礦業有限公司提出的申請，依據《中華人民共和國行政許可法》等相關規定作出，自即日起該意見書依法登出。


江西省自然資源廳有關人士回覆記者稱，這份意見書登出是按照正常流程走下來的，此次只是公告該情況。

據瞭解，《建設專案用地預審與選址意見書》是專案落地的法定前置檔案。一位熟悉土地投資領域的專家告訴記者，該意見書是專案流程中非常靠前的步驟，沒有它，後續手續難以進行，土地也無法使用。意見書登出後，整個專案的後續用地流程需要重新走一遍，專案進度可能會延緩。

另一位業內專家告訴記者，該意見書登出意味著專案前期空間規劃合規性審查的終止，其法律意義在於解除土地用途的預審約束。《建設專案用地預審與選址意見書》是《中華人民共和國土地管理法實施條例》規定的法定前置檔案，需在立項前完成。該意見書在申請過程中，環評、採礦權等手續可同步推進前期準備工作，但土地使用權證辦理必須取得該意見書。

關於企業主動申請登出的原因，該專家分析，通常有三種可能：專案規劃重大調整（如選址變更）；土地指標無法落實；投資主體或建設內容變更。登出後，企業可以立即重新申報，但需要重新履行全部審批流程，辦理時間受地方政務效率影響，通常需要15至30個工作日（含公示期）。他還特別提示，在新意見書辦結之前擅自採礦，屬於《中華人民共和國礦產資源法》禁止的“未批先採”行為。（上證報）

【我國研究提出基於微氣泡強化淬冷的不溶性硫磺製備技術 萃取後的產品IS含量高達97.33%】不溶性硫磺（IS）因其分散均勻、自粘性高等特性，被廣泛應用於高效能綠色輪胎生產領域。近日，中國科學院青島生物能源與過程研究所研究團隊開發出基於微氣泡技術的新型快速淬冷方法。研究團隊研製的旋流式絕熱噴射器採用不鏽鋼與玻璃複合結構設計，透過軸向引入高溫氣態硫與切向注入淬冷液的協同作用，實現溫差極大的氣液兩相高效對流傳熱。該裝置使高溫硫蒸氣在毫秒級接觸時間內分散為微小氣泡，直接生成粒度均勻的IS粉末產品，同時省去傳統工藝的粉碎工序。研究團隊以研製的新型旋流式絕熱噴射器為關鍵淬冷裝備，系統地考察了工藝條件、穩定劑種類和新增量等對產品IS含量及105℃下熱穩定性的影響。結果表明，萃取後的產品IS含量高達97.33%，充油型產品IS的熱穩定性達到86.49%。該旋流式絕熱噴射器淬冷效果優良，為高品質不溶性硫磺的連續化工業生產提供了理想淬冷裝備。相關研究成果發表於《過程工程學報》。研究工作得到國家科技重大專項的支援。

Swedish Household Spending Rebounds in May — Household spending in Sweden increased 0.7% month-on-month in May 2026, rebounding from a 0.8% drop in the previous month. Consumption recovered for housing and utilities (1.4% vs -0.1% in April), transportation (1.3% vs -5.5%), and restaurants and hotels (1% vs -0.1%). At the same time, spending declined at a slower pace for clothing and footwear (-0.2% vs -5.8%), recreation and culture (-2.6% vs -5.3%), and miscellaneous goods and services (-1.3% vs -3.8%), while consumption recorded an increase for information and communication (2.6% vs 2.5%). On the other hand, spending moderated for food and non-alcoholic beverages (1.3% vs 1.8%) and dropped for alcoholic beverages and tobacco (-0.4% vs 1.7%) and healthcare (-4.5% vs 3.1%). On a yearly basis, household consumption advanced 3.4% in May, following a 1.8% rise in the preceding period.

Sweden New Orders Rise in April — Total orders received by the Swedish industry rose by 6.3% year-on-year in April 2026, rebounding from a revised 7.2% decline in the previous month. Orders from foreign customers recovered sharply, rising 9.8% after a 14.1% drop in March, while domestic orders grew at a slower pace of 0.5% from 6.0%. Among industrial groupings, intermediate goods (1.0% vs -25.2%), and durable consumer (4.5% vs -0.8%) rebounded. Growth also accelerated in capital goods (10.0% vs 2.0%). Meanwhile, gains moderated for energy-related goods (15.0% vs. 19.8%) and non-durable consumer goods (8.8% vs. 11.0%). On a monthly basis, industrial orders rose 5.1% in April, recovering from a 0.9% decline in March. In the first four months of the year, total orders were down 1.6%, as domestic demand increased 2.5% while export orders fell 3.9%.

### 2026-06-10 14:24:20  #Trading Economics #Economy #Egypt #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Egypt Inflation Rate at 3-Month Low of 14.6% — Egypt’s annual urban inflation slowed to 14.6% in May 2026 from 14.9% in April, slightly above market expectations of 14.5%. It was the lowest inflation rate since February, as the impact of higher fuel prices continued to fade. Egypt raised fuel prices in early March by about 14% to 17% across a wide range of petroleum products, the first increase this year, amid a surge in oil prices. On a monthly basis, the CPI rose 1.6%, accelerating from a 1.1% increase in the previous month.

### 2026-06-10 14:22:14  #Trading Economics #Economy #Sweden #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Sweden Industrial Output Growth at 7-Month High — Industrial production in Sweden grew by 7.1% year-on-year in April 2026, accelerating from an upwardly revised 3.4% rise in the previous month. This marked the strongest growth in industrial activity since last September, driven largely by a rebound in mining output, which rose 2.3% after falling 6.4% in March, as metal ore mining returned to growth (0.6% vs -7.2%), while contractions in other mining and quarrying activities eased (-1.1% vs -1.2%). Additionally, manufacturing production climbed by 7.2% (vs 3.8%), marking its fastest rise in seven months, supported mainly by higher output for coke and refined petroleum products (18.9% vs 19.4%), chemical and pharmaceutical products (12.6% vs 20.8%), and other non-metallic mineral products (8.9% vs 1.9%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, industrial output rose by 4.2% in April, rebounding from a downwardly revised 1.6% drop in the preceding period.
